   	ceṉṉi - 600 006,  maṟṟum pirācacu intiyā, 
tiruvallikkēṇi - 600 005.
yāṉ aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷil ārāyntu kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷi nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyirukkiṉṟēṉ.
pāṟkaṭalik kaṭintu amutam eṭuttatu pōla, uṇmi kāṇum uṇarcciyiṉāl naṭunili niṉṟu ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ. 
oruvar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kaṟṟālum aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmikaḷi ellām eḷitāka eḻuti vittirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ellārum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaṟkum tolliyi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya tilli, atu muṭiyavum muṭiyātu, tēviyumilli. 
ellā nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa ciṟanta uṇmikaḷi ellām piḻi cāṟāka yāṉ vaṭittut tantuḷḷēṉ.
eṉ nūlkaḷip paṭittāṟpōtum, ataṉāl, tamiḻ muḻutuṅ kaṟṟa payaṉi aṭiyalām.
- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ



cāṉṟitaḻ
nāki - tiru. vētācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ orupōtu tiruvaṉantapurattil oruvāran taṅkiyirunta ñāṉṟu, avaruṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppuk kiṭittataṟkuc cālavum makiḻkiṉṟēm. varuṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷukku maṭṭaṟṟa tamiḻppulami mikuntu varumeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka ivar iḷamiyilēyē nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam uṭiyarāyiruppatu kaḷippūṭṭukiṟatu. maṟṟum tāymoḻip paṟṟuk kuṟinta ikkālattil, ivar tamatu nuṇṇaṟivit tamiḻ nūlkaḷil muṟṟum celutti naṉkārāyntuḷḷatum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. pattuppāṭṭu, kalittoki, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ārāycciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ivartam aṟivuṭimiyē eṉṉi ivarpāl matippuṟumāṟu ceytatu. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ toki curuṅkivarum ikkālattil ivarukkuc ciṟanta ūkkamaḷittāl ivar oru ciṟanta tamiḻāciriyarāvāreṉṟu nām uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟēm. muṉṉarē iraṇṭāntarak kallūrikaḷil tamiḻkaṟpikkap pōtumāṉa aḷavu ilakkaṇa ilakkiyattiṟ ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟavarāyirukkiṉṟāreṉak karutukiṉṟōm. Fiwªj msÉš v~¥, V., (F.A.) vakuppukkuriya āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟārāṉāl mēlnāṭṭu muṟippaṭi tamiḻ nūlkaḷi varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuṭaṉ ārāyavum eḻutavum vallunarāvāreṉpatu emmuṭiya ciṟanta eṇṇam.

vaṇṇārappēṭṭi	maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntarampiḷḷi
tirunelvēli	tamiḻttāy vāḻttiṉ  āciriyar
2-12-1895
(inta naṟcāṉṟu tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil cuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ pērāciriyarāka iruntapōtu koṭukkap peṟṟatu.)

maṟimaliya māṇpu
nuḻivuri
taṉittamiḻ eṉṟa aḷavil muṉ niṟkum muḻuturu eḻilvaṭivar
tavattiru. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē āvar!
munti aṭikaḷ ākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, `muttamiḻ aṭikaḷ eṉa muḻakkamiṭa niṉṟār!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷō, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum takavārnta perumi peṟṟār.
tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyatu eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻit tuṇiyiṉṟi iyaṅkātu eṉṟum, ulaka moḻikaḷuk kellām tāymoḻi vaṭamoḻiyē eṉṟum  tamiḻk kalvi valla peṟṟiyarum muṟṟum mayaṅki yurikka,
vaṭamoḻi ulaka moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi eṉṟāl, ataṟkum maṟṟi moḻikaḷukkum tanti moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟiya vaṭalūr aṭikaḷār pukaḻparappāḷarāyp potunilik kaḻakam kaṇṭa tava aṭikaḷār, taṉittamiḻt tanti eṉṉum perumi peṟṟa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āvar.
civaṉiyam cārnta perumakkaḷum, pēraṟivarkaḷum kūṭa, oppārāy, `taṉittamiḻ (taṉittu amiḻ) eṉṟu paḻikkavum iḻikkavum, tuṇintu niṉṟu, avar veḷka; veḷḷiṭi maliyākat `taṉittamiḻ eṉpati nili nāṭṭiya niṟimali, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārē!
civaṉiya maṭattiṉarum, vaṭamoḻi vallārum, `tol kāppiyak  kaṭal eṉap pukaḻappaṭṭārumām cāmināta tēcikar, nāṇamiṉṟi, 
ainteḻuntāl orupāṭi eṉṟu aṟiyavē nāṇuvar
  aṟivuṭiyōrē; yāmum atuvē
eṉṟum,
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭō?
eṉṟum - eḻutip parappiṉārāka,
oru col tāṉum vēṟṟuc col viravā nūl yām ceyvēm eṉṟu aimpāṉ nāṉku nūlkaḷ kalimali pēḻiyāy eḻutik kāṭcip paṭuttiya māṭciyar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār!
tolkāppiyak kāval
tamiḻ moḻikku vāytta tolkāppiya vaḷanūl,
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
  eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē	(884)
eṉṟum,
moḻi peyarppuc ceytalilum muṟimi pēṇa vēṇṭum eṉpati,
moḻipeyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal	(1597)
eṉṟum,
pēccu vaḻakkil oru kāl vēṟṟuccol vantu viṭiṉum,
marapunili tirital ceyyuṭ killi 	(1590)
eṉṟum - varampāṇi ceytum, ac cevviyal vaḻittaṭam citintum cīraḻintum, cēṟum cakatiyum nāṟum ciṟumiyum kuṟumiyum vaḻivaḻiyē eyta mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy, ayalōr cūḻcciyālum tiṟavōr mayakkālum ēṟpaṭṭa aḻipāṭukaḷi ellām tāmē ōr iyakkamāy iruntu, viḻippum eḻucciyum ūṭṭiyavar maṟimali aṭikaḷē eṉpatāl,
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppa taṟkāka arum pāṭupaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa marapōṭu yāṉ kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu eṉṉum pāvāṇar,
mūvā yiramāṇṭu mōtum vaṭamoḻiyāl
 cāvām takiniṉṟa taṇṭamiḻi - mēvāk
 kumarit taṉinilikkuk koṇṭuvanta  appar
 amarar maṟimali yār
eṉap pāṭip paraviṉār. `taṉit tamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟa pārāṭṭum ip pāṭṭiṉ talippil vaḻaṅkiṉār.
nuṇṇiya nūlpalavum kaṟpatē nōṉpāka
 eṇṇiya vāḻnāḷ elāmaḷittup - paṇṇiya
 centamiḻnūl ovvoṉṟum ceppum kaṭalaṟivi
 pintamiḻ kākkum paṭi
eṉa aṭikaḷ kalvi mēmpāṭṭiyum kalit tiṟap paṭippukaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭiyum pāvēntar pāratitācaṉār pārāṭṭukiṟār.
paltuṟi āṟṟal
aṭikaḷiṉ paltuṟi āṟṟali 1. pērāciriyar, 2. perum pulavar, 3. pāvalar, 4. ārāycciyāḷar, 5. mummoḻip pulavar, 6. moḻipeyarppāḷar, 7. coṟpoḻi vāḷar, 8. eḻuttāḷar, 9. palkalic celvar, 10. taṉittamiḻt tantiyār eṉṟu pāvāṇar pakuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
mēlum avar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulatti
paṉimali mukaṭṭiṉ uyarvum, nīla āṟṟiṉ nīḷamum amiti vāriyiṉ āḻamum amintavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉkiṟār. (paṉimali - imayam; nīla āṟu - nil; amitivāri - pacipikku mākaṭal).
aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷ
aṭikaḷ paṉmāṇ māttamiḻ nūlkaḷi, nūlaka iyakkat tanti araṅka nātarum, innāḷ civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻaka āṭciyarum annāḷ nūlakarumākiya tirumiku irā. muttuk kumāracāmi avarkaḷum 14 vakimippaṭutti vittami perum pārāṭṭukku uriyatām avi,
1. maruttuvam  2. maṟiporuḷ  3. ilakkiyam 4. itaḻkaḷ  5. caṅka  ilakkiyam  6. pāṭal 7. nāṭakam  8. putiṉam 9. kaṭitam 10. kaṭṭuri 11. camayam  12. tattuvam 13. varalāṟu 14. camūka iyal eṉpaṉa.
mutaṟ kuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉṉum maṟuppu nūlē, aṭikaḷiṉ mutal nūlāka veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu (1898) annūṉ maṟuppu nayamē nāki aṭikaḷi - pallavapura aṭikaḷāka -taṉittamiḻ aṭikaḷāka ākka arum perum tūṇṭal āyiṟṟām.
aṭikaḷār varalāṟu: kīḻkaṭal cār nākappaṭṭiṉattic cārnta ciṟṟūr kāṭampāṭi, āṅku vāḻnta aruntavap peruntakiyar cokkanātar - ciṉṉammiyār! ivviṇiyar koṭiyāyt tamiḻaṉṉi ceyta tavap pēṟṟāl piṟantavar vētācalam. piṟanta peru maṅkala nāḷ ki.pi. 15.07.1876.
cokkanātar, cittamalam aṟuvitta cittamaruttuvar; aṟiva maruttuvam eṉpatum atu. attoḻil avarkku vaḷatti vaḻaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl vētācalam kuṟivaṟa vāḻntār.
nākappaṭṭiṉattu veciliyaṉ micaṉ paḷḷiyil aintu vakuppuvari payiṉṟār; ciṟanta māṇavarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. āṟām vakuppil cērum niliyil, arumit tantiyār iyaṟki eytiṉār. āyiṉum aṉṉiyār uṟutiyāl kalviyit toṭarntār. appaḷḷiyil āṅkilak kalvikku irunta mutaṉmi tamiḻukku illi. kiṟittava camayam paṟṟi aṉṟic civaṉiyam paṟṟi aṟiya vāyppilli eṉṉum iraṇṭu kuṟikaḷiyum iḷamiyilēyē uṇarntār.
vīṭṭilēyē tamiḻ nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu mutaṟ kuṟiyiyum, nākiyil irunta intumatāpimāṉa caṅkam vaḻi iraṇṭām kuṟiyiyum nīkkiṉār. paḷḷiyilum kuṟiyaṟak kaṟṟār. varuvāyk kuṟiyāl vāṭum aṉṉikkuc cumiyāy illāmal taṉiyē payilvatē iṉiyavaḻi eṉa eṇṇi, nākiyil puttaka vaṇikarāka viḷaṅkiya nārāyaṇa cāmiyār pulamiyālum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyavar. ātalāl avariṭam taṉiyē ilakkiya  - ilakkaṇa - camaya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟār. nārāyaṇariṉ muṉṉi māṇavar pērā. maṉōṉmaṇīyam pe. cuntaraṉār eṉpatiyum aṟintār. oṉpatām vakuppoṭu paḷḷik kalviyi niṟuttit taṉikkalviyē kaṟṟār. tolkāppiyam, toki nūlkaḷ, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirucciṟṟampalak kōvi, iṟiyaṉār akap poruḷ eṉakkaṟṟār.
aṭikaḷār iyaṟṟiya tiruvoṟṟi murukar mummaṇik kōvi mukappil, ematu patiṉintām āṇṭil muṟippaṭi tuvaṅkiya tamiḻp payiṟciyāṉatu ematu irupattōrām āṇṭiṟkuḷ tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ, ciṟṟampalak kōviyār eṉṉum mūṉṟu nūlkaḷiyum muḻutum neṭṭuruc ceytu muṭittēm eṉṟum vēṟucila nūlkaḷiyum muḻuvatāka ōtiyatiyum kūṟi,
iṅṅaṉamāka, viḻumiya tamiḻppaḻa nūlkaḷil ematu karuttu īrppuṇṭu niṉṟu payiṉṟa payiṟciyiṉālēyē ceyyuḷum uriyum taṉic centamiḻ naṭiyil eḻutum tiṟam ematu iḷamip paruvattilēyē emakku vāyppa tāyiṟṟu eṉkiṟār. avvāṇṭu ki.pi. 1897. nāki intu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṟkuc civa cittānta caṇṭa marutam eṉṉum virutu peṟṟa cōmacuntara nāyakar vantu coṟpoḻivāṟṟiṉār. avar poḻivil vanta karuttoṉṟaṉi maṟuttu oruvar eḻuta, ataṟku maṟuppāka murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉipeyaril maṟuppuri eḻutiṉār. ataṉikkaṟṟa nāyakar, mīḷap poḻivukku varuṅkāl muruka vēḷic cantikka virumpiṉār. accantippiṉ payaṉē uṉṉi virivilē ceṉṉikku varuvippōm. nī antap pakkaṅkaḷil iruppatē nallatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ituvē aṭikaḷārkkuc ceṉṉi vāḻvu amiya mutal tūṇṭal āyiṟṟu. aṭikaḷukkup patiṉēḻām vayatil (1893) tirumaṇam nikaḻntatu. tuṇivi tam māmaṉ makaḷ cavuntara valli eṉpār.
tiruvaṉantapuram
nārāyaṇacāmi avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉi māṇavarām pērā. cuntaraṉār tiruvaṉanta purattil iruntār. avariyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇīya nāṭaka nūl kaṟṟu ataṉ arumi kaṇṭu, pērāciriyarik kāṇavirumpiṉār. ataṉāl tam āciriyarōṭu tiruvaṉantapuram ceṉṟu pērāciriyarōṭu uriyāṭiṉār. vētācalaṉār iḷam paruvattiyum mūtaṟiviyum aṟinta cuntaraṉār viyappuṟṟut takutic cāṉṟu oṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉār. ciṟitu kālam tiruvaṉanta purattil āciriyap paṇiyum ceytār. avviṭattiṉ paruvanili ovvāmiyāl nākikkē tirumpiṉār.
ceṉṉi: cōmacuntaraṉār muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi vētācalaric ceṉṉikku aḻittu aṟiñar nallucāmi eṉpār naṭāttiya cittānta tīpiki eṉṉum itaḻāciriyar ākkiṉār. piṉṉē ceṉṉi kiṟittavak kallūrikku oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar vēṇṭiyiruttal aṟinta cōmacuntaraṉār vētācalaṉā ra akkallūrip pērāciriyarākka muyaṉṟār. atukāl tamiḻttuṟit talivarāka iruntavar vi.kō cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiriyār eṉṉum paritimāl kaliñar. kallūri mutalvar villiyam millar eṉpār.
9.3.1898 il vētācalaṉār paṇiyēṟṟār. aṅkup patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār.
akkallūrik kālattē iḷaṅkali māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka irunta mullippāṭṭu (1903) paṭṭiṉappāli (1906) eṉpavaṟṟukku ārāycci uri eḻutiṉār.
avvuri nūli vāṅkik kaṟṟa māṇākkarum ēṉit tamiḻaṟiñarum putumuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa avvurip pāṅkiṉip piṉpaṟṟip paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷi ārāyvatil kiḷarcci peṟalāṉār. ipputumuṟi uri, tamiḻ nūlārāyum vakikaḷi iṉitu viḷakki aṟiñar palarkkum aka makiḻcci yiṉi viḷittami kaṇṭu iṉi, ittakiya urikaḷiyē eḻututal vēṇṭum eṉa ematuḷḷam peritum viḻintatu eṉkiṟār.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattārāṅkilamum vaṭa moḻiyum kaṭṭāyamākap paṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, tāymoḻip pāṭatti viruppap pāṭamākak kaṟpikkalām eṉṟum oru tīrmāṉam āṭcikkuḻuvil koṇṭu vantaṉar. oru pāṭam viruppappāṭam eṉappaṭṭa piṉṉar ataṉik kaṟka varuvār evar? tamiḻ kaṟkum māṇavar eṇṇikki kuṟiyavē āciriyap paṇiyiṭamum kuṟiya vēṇṭuvatāki, aṭikaḷār 31.1. 1911 ām nāḷuṭaṉ kallūrip paṇiyi muṭikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
māṇavar: ikkālattil aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarāka iruntāruḷ, taṇikimaṇi ceṅkalvarāyar, iracikamaṇi ṭi.kē.ci, nāvalar ca.cō. pārati, pēraṟiñar ca. viyāpuriyār mutalōr cilar.
taṇikimaṇiyiṉ, tēvāra tiruvācakac collaṭivu oru piṟappaḷavil ceyyak kūṭiyatā?
nāvalar ca. cō. pāratiyāriṉ āyvut tuṇivu ettaṉi kacaṭukaḷi nīkkiyatu? viyāpuriyār, kāla āyvilum, vaṭamoḻip piṭivātattilum tammic cālpu āyvil iruntu vilakkiṉālum, caṅka ilakkiyap patippu, nikaṇṭuppatippu, palkalik kaḻaka akaramutalip patippu ivaṟṟic ceyta arumi eṉṉa? puṟattiraṭṭu, kaḷaviyal, kāriki āyavaṟṟiṉ patippuc ciṟappu eṉṉa?
ivarkaḷ paṇiyum aṭikaḷārit taḻuvutal uṇmi tāṉē!
camaraca caṉmārkkam
kallūrip paṇiyil iruntu vīṭupeṟṟa aṭikaḷār, pallavapuratti vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭār. camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam tōṟṟuvittār. 
ellāc cātiyārkkum ellāc camayattārkkum aṅkī kāramāṉa cīvakāruṇiya oḻukkattiyum, camaraca caṉmārk kattiyum piṟkālattil mika valiyuṟuttip pōtitta āciriyar srīmat irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷē āvār. accuvāmikaḷ vali yuṟuttiya avviraṇṭu koḷkikaḷiyum eṅkum valiyuṟutti viḷakki aṉparkaḷi oruṅku kūṭṭa vēṇṭi accuvāmikaḷ iṭṭa peyarālēyē, `camaraca caṉmārkka niliyam eṉṉum itu tāpikkalāyiṟṟu. inniliyattiṟku muṉ āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār; piṉ āciriyar irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ āvar eṉṟu peyarīṭu ceytu koḷkikaḷiyum vakukkiṟār.
kuṭumpam
1898il aṭikaḷār ceṉṉikku varumpōtu avartam aṉṉiyār ciṉṉammiyār, tuṇiviyār cavuntiram ammi yār, celvi cintāmaṇi, aṭikaḷār eṉṉum nālvarik koṇṭa kuṭumpamāka iruntatu.
1911il pallava purattil kuṭipukum pōtu nīlāmpiki, tiruñāṉa campantam, māṇikkavācakam, tirunāvukkaracu, cuntara mūrtti eṉṉum makkaḷum koṇṭa oṉpatiṉmar kuṭumpamāka iruntatu. pallavuram pukunta piṉ tiripura cuntari piṟakkap patiṉmaṉ kuṭumpam āyiṟṟu. inniliyilēyē 27.08.1911il civat tuṟaviyarkkuriya tuṟavāṭi (kāvi) koṇṭu cuvāmi vētācalam āṉār. cuvāmi vētācalam eṉpataṉ taṉittamiḻākkamē maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. muṉṉēyē avari aṭikaḷ eṉṟatu avartam māṇpu karutiyēyām.
mīḷpārvi
nām mīḷpārvi oṉṟupārkka vēṇṭum.
aṭikaḷ 21ām akaviyilēyē taṉittamiḻ āyvuttiṟam kivarap peṟṟār.
1903, 1907 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil mullippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappāli ākiya nūlkaḷukku āyvuri vaḻaṅkuṅkāl itiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ ittaṉi; ivaṟṟuḷ centamiḻccol ittaṉi; vēṟṟuc col ittaṉi - eṉa aṟutiyiṭukiṟār.
ātalāl, taṉittamiḻ eṉṉum tōṟṟam 1916 ām āṇṭil nīlāmpikiyār tūṇṭalāl ēṟpaṭṭami eṉpatu, akattē amintu kiṭanta vittu, muḷi viṭṭu veḷippaṭṭa niliyām. av veḷippaṭṭa niliyum uṭaṉē paṟṟip paṭaravilli! mēlum cila āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉarēyē camacara caṉmārkka niliyam, potunilik kaḻakamākavum, ñāṉacākaram aṟivuk kaṭalākavum, cuvāmi vētācalam maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ ākavum paṭippaṭiyē kiḷarkiṉṟaṉavām!
ōr ariya amippu uruvākum niliyum, ōr ariya kaṇṭu piṭippu ulakavar aṟiya veḷippaṭalum, avi iyakkamākavum payaṉākavum ākiṉṟa niliyum paṟṟiya viḷakkam, aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakka varalāṟṟil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatām!
etiriṭit tūṇṭal: mēlum oṉṟu: oru nēriṭit tūṇṭaliṉum etiriṭit tūṇṭal valimi mikkatu eṉpatum, uḷḷattu uṟutiyi veḷḷap perukkāy veḷippaṭutta vallatu eṉpatum teḷivām.
oṉṟē āyiṉum taṉittamiḻ uṇṭā? eṉṟu kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar oruvarē viṉāvi nakittal, peṟṟa tāy moḻip piṟavikku uṟṟa takimi ākātu eṉpatum, ataṉi uṇarnta appiṟavi pāmpaṉ kurupāta aṭikaḷ pōl cēntaṉ centamiḻ eṉṉa oru nūliyaṟṟit tamiḻ taṉittu iyaṅkavalla taṉi uyar moḻi eṉṟu nilināṭṭiyiruppiṉ vaḻipaṭattakka piṟaviyāy amintirukkum! appēṟu ataṟku vāykkāmi,
  tuṟantārkkut tuppuravu vēṇṭi maṟantārkol 
  maṟṟi yavarkaḷ tavam	(kuṟaḷ 263)
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar vāy moḻippaṭi,
taṉittamiḻt tantip pēṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaṭku vara iruppataṟkē nēriṭṭa nērcciyām eṉka.
iyakkam
aṭikaḷ iyakkam ālamarameṉak kālpala koṇṭu akala akala viriyat taṉittamiḻ iyakkamāy, ulakattamiḻk kaḻakamāy, pāvalarēṟu tamiḻk kaḷamāy, cērar koṟṟamāy, tamiḻkkāppuk kaḻakamāy, tamiḻp pātukāppuk kaḻakamāy, taṉittamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḻakamāy, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakamāy, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ maṉṟamāy, tamiḻkkāval kuḻuvāy, iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakamāy, pāvāṇar tamiḻ iyakkamāy āṅkāṅku mukiḻttu muṉippoṭu kaṭaṉāṟṟa ēviyatām.
nūlakam
aṭikaḷ nūṟṟoki, arumaṇik kuviyaṉṉa nālāyiram nūlkaḷik koṇṭatu. etiratu uṇarntu, civacittānta nūṟ patippuk kaḻakattiṟku muṟkoṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatāl, nālāyiram nāṟpatāyiramāy mēlum mēlum virintu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlakap peyarāl, perumimiku vaḷḷalār mutaṟkaṇ poḻivu ceyta periyavar cōmu avarkaḷ illamē vāykka ulaka vaḷamākkiyatām! ataṉ vaḻiyākavē aṭikaḷ tāmē veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷum, veḷiyiṭāmal kaḻaka vaḻi veḷivanta nūlkaḷum, aṭikaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ veḷivanta nūlkaḷum ākiya muḻumit tokutikaḷum ki.pi. 1898 mutal 1969 vari mutaṟpatippāka veḷivantavi. avaṟṟuḷ paṭikaḷ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyaviyum uṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlkaḷ nāṭṭuṭimi ākkap paṭṭa pēṟṟāl, iṉṟu vāytta nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum oṭṭumottamāka vaḻaṅkum peruvāyppit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peṟṟatām.
veḷḷac cētam
aṇmiyil ēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷap perukku patippakattuḷ pukuntu accu aṭikkappaṭṭatum, aṭikkappaṭa iruntatum, kaṇiṉippaṭutti vitta kuṟuvaṭṭukaḷum,  mūlanūlkaḷum eṉa vāri aḷḷik koṇṭu pōṉami, kaḷattukku vanta katir maṇikaḷ, kaḷañciyattiṟku vārāmal pōya, pēraḻivu oppatām. ātalāl, nūlkaḷi mīḷat tēṭavum, kaṇiṉippaṭuttavum, kuṟuvaṭṭu ākki acciṭavum eṉa mummaṭaṅkup paṇiyum pēriḻappum perutta avalamum uṇṭākkiṟṟām. anniliyil,
  ūḻiyum uppakkam kāṇpar uliviṉṟit
  tāḻā tuñaṟṟu pavar
eṉṉum vāymoḻiyi vāymi moḻiyākkiya tuṇivuc celvar iḷavaḻakaṉārum avar kuṭumpattavarum āvar!
muṉṉaṟivipput tiṭṭappaṭi nūlukku ārvamāyp paṇam celuttiyōrkkum nūl kālattil kiṭṭā uḷiccal illāmal pōkātē! avarkaḷum amintu tāṅkal, iyaṟki iyaṅkiyal vaḻiyatām.
veḷiyīṭukaḷ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻmoḻi iṉa maṇvaḷam - vaḷarcci mīṭṭeṭuppu - eṉpavaṟṟi muṉvittut tamiḻp pōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭatāl, pēriḻappukkām eṉat teḷivākat terintum, icik kalikku vāytta oppaṟṟa kaḷañciyamāy vāytta - karuṇāmirta cākaratti veḷiyiṭṭatām! muṉṉi vanta mutaṟpakuti pēriḻappākkiyum ataṉ piṉṉip pakutikaḷiyum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭatu! moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar; īḻattu aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā;  varalāṟṟaṟiñar cāmināta carmā, urivēntar auvi cu.tu; nāvalar na.mu.vē; paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālar; cāmi citamparaṉār; mayili cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmiyār; pēraṟiñar ka. veḷḷi vāraṇaṉār; pāvalar kuḻantiyār; pāvalar muṭiyaracaṉār; pāvēntar, tol poruḷ kalveṭṭu āyñar catācivaṉār, cāttaṉkuḷam irākavaṉ  nūlkaḷiyum eḷiyēṉ nūlkaḷiyum muḻumi muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyam- pāṭṭu - toki - kāppiyam - kīḻkkaṇakku - tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu - āṅkilam valla aṟiñar irāma nātaṉār nūlkaḷiyum, yāḻppāṇa akarāti mutalām akarātit tokutikaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭatu. patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutitoṭṭu, irupattōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti vari veḷivanta - tamiḻukku ākkamām nāṭ ṭuṭimi ākkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷi ellām - ōriṭattup peṟṟu, oru taṉittamiḻ nūlakam amikkum aḷavukku acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya perumi iḷavaḻakaṉārkku uṇṭu. avarē aṉṟi avar tuṇi yiyum makkaḷiyum uṟaviyum aṉpiyum naṇpiyum oruṅkē iṇittu, aṟiñar peru makkaḷ palar aravaṇippiyum vāyppak koṇṭu vāḻum iyalpu avarkku vāyttalāl maṭṭumē, ivvāṟu ceyaṟkariya ceyya vāyttatām eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻpavarum avar.
maṟimaliya māṇpu
maṟimaliyam, taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! tamiḻt taṉip peruvaḷam! muttamiḻc curappu! cīrtiruttac ceḻumi! vāḻviyal vaḷam! kalic collākkak kaḷañciyam! paṇṭit tamiḻar varalāṟṟup putiyal! āyviyal vaḻikāṭṭi! poḻivup polivu! eḻuttiṉ eḻil! moḻik kāval muṉiyam! muṭṭaṟukkum muḻutuṟu koṭi! mīṭṭeṭukkum mētakimi! aḷḷa aḷḷak kuṟiyā aṇimaṇik kuviyal! eṉal meymi viḷakkamām!
puttakam ēṟṟup polivatē puttakam
 vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
tiruvaḷḷuvar niliyam,						                    iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
7, irāmaṉ teru,							                irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
tirunakar, maturi - 6
625006.

malikka vikkum maṟimaliyam!
patippuri
moḻiyē iṉamakkaḷ muṉṉēṟa nalla
vaḻiyām ataṉi vaḷarkka -paḻiyāyt
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ
moḻippēraṟiñar pāvāṇar avarkaḷ `moḻiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē moḻipēcum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum eṉak kūṟukiṟār. ulakam muḻutum ivvuṇmi uṇarappaṭṭuḷḷati varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇari aṟivulakam cariyāka aṭiyāḷam kāṇpataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉatu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tanta pārāṭṭu!
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ (15.7.1876 - 15.9.1950) tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (7.2.1902 - 16.1.1981) iruvarum taṉittamiḻiyakkattit tāṅki niṟkum tūṇkaḷākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1916il tōṟṟuvitta taṉittamiḻ iyakkam, nūṟṟāṇṭi eṭṭiyuḷḷa makiḻvāṉa nēram itu!
taṉittamiḻiyakka nūṟṟāṇṭi niṉivu kūrum vakiyil, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tanti maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ eḻuttu, coṟpoḻivu, nāṭkuṟippu, kaṭitaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum `maṟimaliyam eṉum peruntokuppākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam vaḻi veḷikkoṇarat tiṭṭamiṭṭōm.
ulakam muḻutum paravi vāḻum uṇarvu kuṉṟāta tamiḻarkaḷ tanta ūkkuvippu, intap peruntiṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟit tīravēṇṭum eṉum uṟutiyi eṉakkut tantatu.
taṉittamiḻ iyakka aṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum aṭikaḷāriṉ ariya paṭippukaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tēṭittēṭi vaḻaṅki vaḻikāṭṭiṉar; valimiyūṭṭiṉar.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār 1898ām āṇṭiliruntu eḻuti vantuḷḷār. aṟivukkaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻi 1902ām āṇṭiliruntu veḷiyiṭṭu vantuḷḷār. avariṉ paṭippukaḷ peruveḷḷamāy veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
accēṟātavi, peyar kūṭak kēṭṭaṟiyātavi, peyar terintum pārvikkuk kiṭṭātavi - eṉap palavakiyāyk kiṭittaṉa aṭikaḷāriṉ arumpaṭippukaḷ! avaṟṟi `maṟimaliyam eṉum talippil 34 tokuppukaḷāka orucērap pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻulakam viyappaṭivatu uṟuti.
moḻinalam iṉanalam pēṇutal aṉṟō
muḻunalam peṟuvaḻi! nāmpeṟal eṉṟō?
ippaṭik kavaliyōṭu kēṭpār pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār (10.3.1933 - 11.6.1995). avariṉ `teṉmoḻi itaḻ 1.8.1959 iliruntu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiya piṉputāṉ, taṉittamiḻt tici nōkki nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr naṭipōṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atil nāṉum oruvaṉ.
peruñcittiraṉāriṉ eḻuttu kāṭṭiya aṭiyāḷattāl, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ eṉum aṟivupputiyal palar kikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyatu.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ valiyuṟuttip pēciyatāl, maṟimali yaṭikaḷārit tēṭippayilum ārvam tamiḻulakil puttuyir peṟṟatu.
āriyatti etirppatilum camaṟkirutatti vilakku vatilumē tamiḻiṉa mēmpāṭum tamiḻk kāppum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. avvuṇarcciyit tamiḻar neñcaṅkaḷil arumpa vittatu tirāviṭar iyakkam; malarntu viriyavittatu taṉittamiḻ iyakkam! kumuka, araciyal, paṇpāṭṭu malarccikku inta īriyakkaṅkaḷiṉ pēruḻippup payaṉpaṭṭatu.
tamiḻakattil taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu; 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ cariyat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil tamiḻ iḻuttuc cellappaṭṭu viṭāmal kāppāṟṟiyōr kamparum pavaṇantiyum! piṉvantōrukku antak kāppuṇarcci illāmal, ayaṉ moḻip paṟṟukku aṭimiyāṉatāl tamiḻum tamiḻiṉamum peṟṟa iḻappukaḷ ērāḷam!
ayaṉmoḻic cēṟṟiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭkum muyaṟci āṅkilēyar varukiyiyoṭṭip puttuyir peṟṟatu. vaḷḷalār, pāmpaṉ cāmikaḷ, vaṇṇac carapam taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiyacivā, paritimāṟ kaliñar mutaliya palarum toṭakka vitikaḷāyiṉar.
kōvi avināciyil piṟantu virutunakaril vāḻnta viruti civañāṉayōki 19.11.1908 ām nāḷ `tiruviṭar kaḻakam toṭaṅkit taṉittamiḻ uṇarccikku vēkaṅkūṭṭiṉār. avvamippil uṟuppiṉarāyiruntavar cuvāmi vētācalam (malimaliyaṭikaḷ).
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉat tam peyarik koṇṭu, 1916il avar toṭaṅkiyatē taṉittamiḻ iyakkam.
`tuṟimiku āṅkilam maṟivaṭa moḻiyoṭu
 tūytamiḻp pēraṟiñar - taṉittamiḻ
 tōṟṟiya ōraṟiñar
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār pāṭiyataṟkēṟpa, maṟimali yaṭikaḷār tamiḻ, āṅkilam, camaṟkirutam mutaliya paṉmoḻiyilum pulami mikkavar.
paṉmoḻip pēraṟiñarāṉa maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum ātaravāka niṉṟatāl etirppukkum iḻappukkum āḷākkappaṭṭār. āṉālum uṟuti kuṉṟāmal tamiḻukku ātaravāy uḻikkum uḷḷattōṭiruntār. ataṉāl, tamiḻar uḷḷaṅkaḷil iṉṟaḷavum māṟāp perumiyōṭu avar vīṟṟirukkiṉṟār.
  vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
  maṟimali yaṭikaḷ maṟiyāt tiruppeyar
  vāḻttāta nāḷilli viyakam
pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avar māṇpiṉip pāṭi makiḻntatum, tam `kuṭumpa viḷakku nūliṉi maṟimali yaṭikaḷukkuk kāṇikkiyākki vaḻaṅkiyatum, avariṉ uṟuti taḷarāta uḷḷamum uḻippum kaṇṭu pōṟṟiya naṉṟik kaṭaṉ eṉpatil ayyamilli.
ceṉṉiyil kaṭanta irupatu āṇṭukaḷākac ceyalpaṭṭu varum tamiḻmaṇ patippakap paṇikaḷit tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
vāḻum talimuṟikkum varuṅkālat talimuṟikkum vaḻikāṭṭavalla tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palariṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumuḻut tokuppukaḷāka vaḻaṅkiyiruppatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ muṭippiṉi (cātaṉi)yākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷi muḻumiyāṉa tokuppukaḷākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu. avi kālam muḻutum payaṉpaṭavalla karuvi nūlkaḷ eṉpati aṟiñar ulakam aṟiyum!
tamatu nalatti otukki vittu, tamiḻ nalattik kākka vāḻnta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉittu nūlkaḷiyum oru cērat tokuttu muḻut tokuppāka vaḻaṅkuvatu perumpaṇi! ataṉit tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭarntu niṟivēṟṟi varukiṟatu. tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalip piṉṉiṇippil kāṇka.
tamiḻukkāka uḻikkum uṇarvi iḷamiyil emmiṭam ēṟpaṭuttiyōruḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉārum avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉārum kuṟippiṭattakkōr! em naṉṟikkuriyōr!
taṉittamiḻ ārvam, tirāviṭar iyakka īṭupāṭu eṉum iru ciṟakukaḷāl emmi eḻa vittavar perumpulavar nakkīraṉār! avari eṇṇik kaṇpaṉikka naṉṟi kūṟukiṟatu em neñcam.
kālappaḻamiyālum parāmarippuk kuṟivālum aḻiya nērnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟavi! avaṟṟil orupakutiyi yāvatu mīṭṭu nam kikaḷukku vaḻaṅkiyōr āṟumukanāvalar, ci.vi. tāmōtaraṉār, u.vē.cāminātar mutaliya cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ!
tamiḻilakkiyap peruñcelvatti mīṇṭum mīṇṭum accākki, aṉivar kikaḷiyum eṭṭac ceyta perumi - civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, u.vē.cā. nūlakam, maṇivācakar patippakam mutaliya patippāḷarkaḷukku uriyatu.
accuttuṟi aṟimukamāṉa piṉpē, aṟivuttuṟi aṉivarukkum potuccottākum vāyppu vantatu. eṭṭāk kaṉiyāka ōlic cuvaṭikaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭanta ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷ, ellōrukkum eḷitāyk kiṭikkum paṇiyic ceyta patippāciriyarkaḷum, patippāḷarkaḷum nam naṉṟik kuriyōr!
iravu pakal pārātu mutumiyik karutātu uḻikkum maṉavuratti muṉṉōṭip patippāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇiyē emakku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
aracum, palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, aṟaniṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum, camaya pīṭaṅkaḷum ,perum iyakkaṅkaḷum, perum celvarkaḷum ceyya vēṇṭiya perun tamiḻppaṇi itu! eḷiyavarkaḷāl iyaṅkum tamiḻmaṇ patippakam peruntamiḻp paṇikaḷi iṉṉalkaḷukku iṭiyil arumuyaṟciyōṭu ceytu varuvatu, uḷḷavuram oṉṟiṉāltāṉ!
  vallami cērkkum valimiyuṇ ṭākkum!
  vaṇṭamiḻ nintiṭil etunammik kākkum?
kākkum tamiḻik kākkum uṇarvip pāvēntar pāṭal varikaḷ tantaṉa.
accukku eṭṭāmalum kikkuk kiṭṭāmalum irunta pēraṟiñar palariṉ nūlkaḷi nāṭṭuṭimiyākki tirāviṭar iyakka aracukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, aṟivu paravalākum vāyppukaḷ uruvāyiṉa.
`carivum iḻappum namakku vantālum tamiḻukku varakkūṭātu eṉa eṇṇi uḻikkum iyakkaṅkaḷum amippukaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaṟṟāmal vaḷarntapaṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa.

tamiḻ malarccikkum taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum aṭittaḷam amitta ittaku amippukaḷukku mūla viciyākat tikaḻum perumiyip peṟuvōr maṟimaliyaṭikaḷārum pāvāṇarumē!
tamiḻ vaḷattiṟkum tamiḻar nalattiṟkum civaṉiya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷappariyavi. avi toṭarpāka avar āyntaḷitta aṟivu muttukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi!

cākuntala nāṭaka ārāycci

1960il civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam patippitta
nūliṉi mūlamākak koṇṭu innūl patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
innūlip paṟṟiya kuṟippuri...
kāḷitācariṉ kālam, tiṟaṉ ākiyavaṟṟi innūlil aṭikaḷār ārāyntuḷḷār. nāṭaka iyalpu, nāṭaka varalāṟu, nāṭaka amippu, nāṭakak kati yamippu, nāṭaka māntar iyaṟki. kati nikaḻum iṭamum kālamum, nāṭaka amippiṟ kāṇappaṭum cila kuṟipāṭukaḷ, kāḷitācar kālappaḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ eṉap pala kūṟukaḷākac cākuntala nāṭaka ārāycci amintuḷḷatu.
innūliṉ nāṭakak kali taṉittum oppīṭṭu niliyilum ārāyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
muṉṉi nāḷ nallicip pulavarkaḷ, ulakiyaṟki, makkaḷ iyaṟkikaḷiṉ uṇmi vaṉappukaḷi uṇarntu uṇarttum meyyaṟivu mēṉmi mikkavarkaḷ. ittaku iyalpiṉāl iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ciṟanta nallicip pulavarāka viḷaṅkukiṟār. kāḷitācariṉ mutirnta pulamiyum, nāṭaka nūl nuṭpaṅkaḷ naṉkuṇarnta uṇarvum kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa vākum. ivvāṟu aṭikaḷāriṉ cākuntala nāṭaka ārāycci aṟivu nalam perukkum ariya ārāycciyākum. aḻakiya iṉiya eḷiya tamiḻ naṭiyiyum innūl koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
- ṭākṭar nā. ceyappirakācu
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ ilakkiyappaṭippukaḷ (pak. 19)


PREFACE
“A play attins artistic perfection just in proportion as it approaches that unity of lyrical effect, as if a sony or ballad were still lying at the root of it, all the various expression of the conflict of character and circumstance calling at last into the compass of a single melody or musical theme.” - Walter Pater.
What Pater, the profound critic and the master of an inimitable English prose style, has sid in the above extract taken from his essay on ‘Shakespeare’s English Kings’ applies closely to the Sakuntala the famous drama of the Sanscrit poet Kalidasa. The theme of the drama is that which, with all the lyrical charm, treats of the deep love that had sprung between Sakuntala who lived in the hermitage of her foster-father removed far away from the busy hunts of men and the king Dushyanta who accidentally visited her there while he was on a hunting campign in the forests at the foot of the Himalayas. The plot of the drama is not variegated, as in the comedies of Shakespeare by any artistically inwoven under plots, but is extremely simple and by means of the passionate love-songs that appear all through and by its easily comprehensible unity produces in effect markedly lyrical. Nevertheless, the interest of the plot rises at the point where the poet complicates the situation by skilfully introducing into it the curse of the sage Durvasa and making it appear for a time as if the unity had been broken by that unforeseen circumstance and by the conflict of character exhibited between an innocent young woman absorbed in her recent love experience and the easily irritated sage who had suddenly appeared before her. Still the lyrical unity runs clearly through the succeeding events by the interest of the plot being attached anew to the loss and recovery of the ring which the king put on the finger of Sakuntala when he returned to his capital. From this it must be evident how artistically the poet Kalidasa has, for the purpose of the play, perfected this simple story taken from the Mahabharata, the great store-house of the Indian legends, by introducing into it the curse    of Durvasa which is not found in its original source but which has been skilfully created by the poet himself. Though the creation of this incident looks admirable, so far as it seves as an indispensable hinge on which the plot turns, I might be permitted to question whether it is not inconsistent with the stinless character of a sintly person. I have discussed this point in the following critical study of the Sakuntala and have shown how Shakespeare stands superior to Kalidasa in this respect.
But in the art of delineating the min and the sub-ordinate characters that appear in this drama kalidasa is not in any way inferior to Shakespeare but to a remarkable degree is his equal. With what great acuteness Kalidasa has penetrated to the inward nature of each character, in what brief, pointed expressions of thought and feeling he has so well brought it out, and in what strong relief he sets one in contrast with or foil for the other, can be grasped only by those who make an attentive and discerning study of the speeches of each personage. To the best of my understanding I have, in the following critique, shown at  length these psychological differences which the poet has so carefully observed and noted in his characters.
Years ago I happened to hear in English lecture delivered by an able brahmin scholar on this drama of Kalidasa. After dwelling on some of the excellences of the play, when he came to speak of the character - study of the poet, he, instead of going right through the speeches manners and actions of each dramatic  personage, and trying to exhibit whether or not there were special features in each, summarily dismissed it by saying that Kalidasa was sadly wanting in his delineation of character and is therefore no peer of Shakespeare the prince of the dramatists. That sweeping remark of the learned lecturer kindled in me a burning desire to make a comparative study of the dramatic art of the two poets, and from that time onward I have been reading critically not only the plays of Kalidasa and Shakespeare but also the commentaries on their plays. Unfortunately in the whole range of either Sanscrit or English Literature I could not meet even a single commentary on Kalidasa’s dramas that is worth mentioning along side of the very interesting and illuminating Shakespearian commentaries produced by Ulrici, Gervinus, Schlegel, Swinburne, Dowden, Brooke, Bradley, Symons, Hudson, Moulton, Corson, Brandes and others. In this life no one can experience a pleasure comparable in its height, amount and intensity to the intellectual and aesthetic pleasure which a study of these celebrated commentaries afford. The pleasure given by these is equal only to that yielded by the plays themselves, nay, I may venture to say it is even a degree higher than that. In this class of brilliant Shakespearian commentaries must be included also J.A. Symond’s critical estimates both of the British dramatists and of the old Greek tragedians Aeschylus, Sophocles and Euripides, since these mark an epoch of higher dramatic criticism.
The great literary pleasure experienced in the study of these Shakespearian commentaries, impelled me to attempt after their model a deep and discriminating study of the dramatic art of Kalidasa, a Tamil translation of whose mature production the Sakuntala I produced in the year 1906 and a second edition of which is now issued with notes and the following critique which embodies the results of my study. This and my similar critical commentaries on the ancient Tamil Idylls the Mullippattu, and the Pattinappali, I believe, may serve to remove to a certin extent the reproach that the modern Tamil literature is sadly barren of higher critical prose writings. If the reader finds this my clim justifiable, I shall consider my labour as sufficiently recompensed.
In the following critique, besides showing the merits of Kalidasa’s dramatic art, I have also fixed his date in the first half of the fifth century A.D. basing it on the indisputable epigraphical evidence and co-ordianting this evidence by that afforded by the work itself.
Finally I am glad to say that, in tracing the origin and growth of the drama in India, I quite accidentally lighted on the discovery that this art took its rise not from among the alien Aryas but from the indigenous Tamils themselves. I believe I have in the following critique sufficiently explined all the facts that led me to this most important discovery.
Pallavaram,	  VEDACHALAM
6-1-1934

1. nāṭaka iyalpu
vaṭamoḻi nallicippulavarāṉa kāḷitāca riyaṟṟiya cākuntala nāṭaka amippiṉ tiṟaṉum nuṭpamum vaṉappum payaṉum ārāyntu teḷivataṟkumuṉ, nāṭakattiṉ iyalpum aḵtu ipparatanāṭṭiṉ kaṭṭōṉṟiya varalāṟun terintu koḷḷal iṉṟiyamiyātatākaliṉ, mutalil nāṭakattiṉ iyalpu iṉṉateṉpataṉi oru ciṟitu viḷakkuvām. āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷum avarallāta ēṉi iyaṅkum uyirkaḷuñ ceyyum muyaṟcikaḷ ellām aṟivu muyaṟciyun toḻiṉ muyaṟciyum eṉṉum iruperum pirivil vantu aṭaṅkiniṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu ellāvuyirkaḷ māṭṭuṅ kāṇappaṭum ivviruvaki muyaṟci kaḷum potuvākat tuṉpa nīkkattiyum iṉpa ākkattiyumē avāvi niṟpaṉavākum. ivvuyirkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum potuvāka uḷḷa tuṉpaṅkaḷ paciyum kāmamumākavē  kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. pacittuṉpatti uṇaviṉālum, kāmattuṉpatti āṇum peṇṇumāy oruṅku maruvutalāṉum pōkki, avvāṟṟāl ellā uyirkaḷum iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntu varutalik kaṇkūṭāka nām kaṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉam allamō? iṅṅaṉamākat tamakku iyaṟkiyē eḻum iruvakit tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum nīkki avvāṟṟāl iruvēṟu iṉpaṅkaḷi nukarum iyaṅkum uyirkaḷ avviruvakiyiṉpa nukarcci yaḷavil amiti peṟāmal, vēṟumōr iṉpatti nukartalil viḻivu mikuntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. aḵtiyātōveṉil, avi tān tām virumpu māṟellām viḷiyāṭi nukarum iṉpamēyām. pārmiṉkaḷ! paciyuṅ kāmanukarcciyun tīrntapiṟaku pūṉi nāy mutaliya uyirkaḷum evvaḷavu kiḷarcciyuṭaṉ viḷiyāṭi iṉpuṟu kiṉṟaṉa! pūṉiyāṉatu maraṅkaḷip piṟāṇṭiyum, patuṅkit tuḷḷiyum viḷiyāṭutali evarum pārttirukkalām. tōḻamikoṇṭa iraṇṭu nāykaḷ poyyāka oṉṟi yoṉṟu kuviyum. oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṉkālkaḷāṟ piṇintum, oṉṟiviṭ ṭoṉṟu appuṟaṅ kutittōṭi mīṇṭu vantu kuviyum, oṉṟi yoṉṟu nōkkiyapaṭiyāyp patuṅkiyum, oṉṟu oṉṟaṉ mēṟ pāyntum iṅṅaṉamellām viḷiyāṭṭayarntu makiḻtali evarum pārttirukkalām. iṉṉum veḷi nilaṅkaḷilum malic cāraliṉ kaṇ uḷḷa paḷḷattākkukaḷilum mēyum āṭumāṭukaḷ pul mēyntapiṉ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭiyum, oṉṟaṉmēloṉṟēṟiyum, oṉṟu turatta maṟṟoṉṟu vālik kiḷappikkoṇṭu ōṭiyum, tuḷḷiyum viḷiyāṭik kaḷittali aṅkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷ  pārttirukkalām.
iṅṅaṉamē makkaḷāyp piṟanta maṉṉuyirkaḷum pala vakiyāṉa viḷiyāṭalkaḷāl tam oḻivu nēraṅkaḷik kaḷippuṭaṉ kaḻittu varukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷallāta maṟṟi yuyirkaḷ pakuttaṟivu illātaṉavākaliṉ, avi tammuṭiya viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷi ōr oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil viḷiyāṭavum, avi tammip piṟavuyirkaḷukku oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil viḷiyāṭik kāṭṭavum  vallaṉa alla. maṟṟu, makkaḷō tamakkuḷḷa pakuttaṟivu vaṉmiyāl tām viḷiyāṭum vakikaḷi ōroḻuṅkupaṭa amittukkoḷvatuṭaṉ, tammō ṭottārkkum avi tammi ōr oḻuṅkuṟak kāṭṭi avarkkup perumakiḻcciyiṉi viḷivittum varukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu makkaḷ ōr oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭalkaḷ attaṉiyum mutalil evvāṟu tōṉṟalāyiṉa veṉiṉ; mutirnta paruvattiṉarāyirukkum makkaḷ tuvakkattil enta niliyiṉarāyiruntu evvāṟu viḷiyāṭiṉareṉpati ārāyavē avitōṉṟiya vuṇmi teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
pārmiṉkaḷ! nām viḷiyāṭum paruvattuc ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷāy irunta ñāṉṟu eṅṅaṉam viḷiyāṭiṉōm? cōṟu camikkum viḷiyāṭṭum, `peṇṇu māppiḷi vittu maṇañ ceyvikkum viḷiyāṭṭum allavō viḷiyāṭiṉam? ēṉic ciṟṟuyirkaḷippōlavē, makkaḷum paci kāmam uṭiyar; eṉṟālum, pacittapōtu oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil uṇavu camittu uṇṇavuṅ, kāmavēṭki yeḻuntapōtu oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil āṇum peṇṇumāka maṇantu kāmaviṉpam nukaravum makkaḷ oḻuṅkāṉa aṟivumuyaṟci vāyntavarāyiruttalpōla, vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ vāyntavākāmi teḷiyakkiṭanta toṉṟām, makkaḷ ceyyum muṟiyāṉa aṟivumuyaṟcikaḷ ellām ivvāṟu camiyaṟṟoḻilum maṇaviṉiyum eṉṉum iraṇṭil vantaṭaṅki oḻuṅkāka naṭipeṟak kāṇṭaliṉ, avar tañciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷun tāmuñ cōṟu camittum peṇṇu māppiḷi vittu maṇampurivittum viḷiyāṭṭu ayarā niṟkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, piṟparuvattil makkaḷ ayarum palvēṟu viḷiyāṭalkaḷuk kellām muṟpaṭṭu mutalāyniṟpaṉa ciṟār ayarum iv viruvaki viḷiyāṭalkaḷēyā meṉpatu teṟṟeṉa uṇarntu koḷḷappaṭu meṉka.
iṉit, tam peṟṟā ruṟṟār ceyyuñ ceyalkaḷik kaṇṭu avaṟṟippōl tāmuñ ceytu viḷiyāṭṭu ayarvatiṟ ciṟumakārkku uṇṭām makiḻcciyiṉ iyalpiṉi āḻntu ārāyuṅkāl, ōr oḻuṅkāṉa amippiyum oru kiḷarcciyāṉa ceyaliyuṅ kaṇṭu avipōṟṟāmuñ ceytalilum, allatu avipōṟ ceytu kāṭṭappaṭuṅ kāṭci yilumāka makkaḷ peritum iṉpuṟuntaṉmiyareṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākāniṟkiṉṟatu. ciṟumakār tam peṟṟōr ceyyuñ camiyaṟ ṟoḻilip pārttut tāmum maṇaṟcōṟu camittu iṉpuṟutal pōlavē āṇṭu mutirntavarkaḷun tammāṟ pārāṭṭappaṭuvār ceyyum nalviṉi tīviṉikaḷip pārttu avarpōṟ ṟāmuñ ceytu iṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. kalviyaṟiviluñ col vaṉmiyiluñ ciṟantār oruvar ōravikkaḷattēṟik kaṭavuḷiṉ aḷaviṟanta āṟṟaliyum avar eṇṇiṟanta ulakaṅkaḷiyellām vāṉveḷiyiṟ pantukaḷpōl vittuc cuḻaṟṟutaliyum av vulakaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum uḷḷa eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷukkum eṇṇiṟanta viyattakum uṭampukaḷik koṭuttu avar avi tamakku aṟivuviḷaṅkac ceytuvarum aruṭṭiṟaṅkaḷiyum, nām  nam kaṇṇetirē kāṇpatupōl eṭutturittu nam aṟiviyum aṉpiyuṅ kavarntu ceṉṟakkāl, nām ataṉāl avar vayamāki avarippōṟ pēcavum avarip pōṟ kikāl acikkavum viḻikiṉṟaṉam allamō? avviḻivu mītūrap peṟṟakkāl avarpōl avikaḷic ceyyak kaṟṟu nām makiḻtalum uṇṭaṉṟō? iṅṅaṉam nalviṉi ceyvārik kaṇṭu nāmum avar pōṟ ceyyavuñ ceytu makiḻavum muṉintu niṟṟal pōlavē, tīviṉi ceyvārik kaṇṭum avarpōṟ ceyyavuñ ceytu makiḻavum muṉintu  niṟpārum ulakil mikap palarā yirukkiṉṟaṉar. kaṭavuḷ illiyeṉṟum, ellām iyaṟkiyē yeṉṟum, ūṉ uṇṭal, kaṭkuṭittal, vari tuṟiyiṉṟi maruval, ēmāntavar poruḷik kavartal, ēmāṟātavariyum ēmāṟṟutal, aṟivil ēṟṟat tāḻviṉmi yiṉ oruvar ēṉiyoruvarkku aṭaṅkā toḻukutal mutaliya viṉikaḷiyellām avaravar tattam vaṉmi meṉmikaṭ kēṟpac ceytal kuṟṟa mākāteṉṟum āravārat tōṭu uriyāṭuvār tiṟattik kaṇṭu avari viyappārum, avar pōṟceyya virumpu vārum, ceytoḻukuvārum varavara mikutal kāṇṭumaṉṟō? iṉṉum oru malic cārali yum ataṉ kīḻuḷḷatōr ērik kariyil māṉmantikaḷ nīrarunti niṟṟaliyuṅ kaṇṭu makiḻum makiḻcciyiviṭa, ak kāṭciyiṉi eḷḷaḷavum picakāmal varintu vittirukkum ōr ōviyattiṉik kaṇṭu ataṉi viyantu makiḻum makiḻcci nampāl mikap peritā yirukkiṉṟa taṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamē, nām ōr aracaṉiyum, avaṉ araci yiyum, avaṉṟaṉ amiccar paṭittalivar paṭiyāṭ kaḷiyum, avaṉi etirkkum avaṉṟaṉ māṟṟaracar avaṉuk kuriyārkaḷ mutalāyiṉāriyuṅ kāṇa nērum pōtum, avar uriyāṭuvaṉavaṟṟik kēṭka nērum pōtum, avar ceyyuñ ceyalkaḷip pārkka nērum pōtum accattoṭu viraviya oruvaki makiḻcciyiṉi aṭikiṉṟaṉarēṉum, am makiḻcci, avarkaḷippōṟ kōlampūṇṭu avarkaḷip pōl uriyāṭi avarkaḷip pōl naṭamāṭikkāṭṭum nāṭakakkārarik kaṇṭu nām aṭiyum makiḻccikku īṭāka māṭṭātu. ākavē, uṇmiyāka uḷḷa oru tōṟṟat tiṉiyum, uṇmiyāka nikaḻum oru nikaḻcciyiṉiyuṅ kaṇṭu eytum iṉpattiṉum avaṟṟip pōlvaṉavān tōṟṟattiyum nikaḻcciyiyuṅ kaṇṭu eytum iṉpamē makkaḷuḷḷattip perituṅ kavarum peṟṟiyatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉa ōrntukoḷka.
iṉṉum, uṇmiyāṉatoru tōṟṟattiyum nikaḻcci yiyuṅ kaṇṭu kaḷikkuṅkālum, nām avaṟṟik kāṇum avvaḷavil amitipeṟāmal, avaṟṟōṭotta aḻakiya tōṟṟaṅkaḷiyum nikaḻccikaḷiyum annērattil uṭaṉ niṉintō niṉintu colliyō mēlum mikak kaḷitta lilē mika muṉintu niṟkiṉṟaṉam allamō? tiruttamāṉa amippum poṉniṟamum vāyntu tikaḻum oru maṅkiyik kāṇpavar, avaḷit tām kaṇṭu makiḻuṅkāl ā! ivaḷ oru poṟpāviyi yottup polikiṉṟāḷ! eṉṟu avaḷukku oru poṟpāviyi oppāka eṭuttuc colli viyantu makiḻtalik kāṇṭumallamō? piṉṉēyuḷḷa cākuntala nāṭakattiluñ cakuntaliyiṉ vaṭivaḻakik kaṇṭa tuṣiyanta maṉṉaṉ,
mōvā malarō nakaṅkaḷi yāta muḻumuṟiyō!
āvā karuvi turuvātu peṟṟa arumaṇiyō!
nāvāṟ cuviyāp putunaṟa vō! ceyta naṟṟavaṅkaḷ
tāvā toruṅku tiraṇṭuvan tālaṉṉa tiyalarē!
eṉṟu avaḷatu putteḻil nalattiṉ iyalpukku otta pala arumperum putup poruḷkaḷi uvamiyāka eṭuttuc collip peritu makiḻtalkāṇka. ivvāṟē, maṉak kavarcciyiṉi viḷikkum enta uyarnta tōṟṟatti enta aḻakiya poruḷik kaṇṭālum, avaṟṟōṭotta piṟaporuṭṭōṟṟattiyum aḻakiyum oruṅku niṉintu makiḻtalē makkaḷuḷḷattiṉ iyaṟki nikaḻcciyākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. innikaḻcciyiṉ mutaṟṟōṟṟamē ciṟāriṭattu maṇaṟcōṟṟu viḷiyāṭṭākavum marappāvi maṇamākavum mukiḻkkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, ulakanaṭiyil mutaṉmiyuṟṟut tōṉṟum iruvaki nikaḻccikaḷi iṅṅaṉam oppumi vakiyāl uḷḷaṭakkiya ivviruvakic ciṟār viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḷiliruntē, immalar taliyulakiṟ palatalimuṟiyāyp parantu virinta nikaḻccikaḷi yellāṅ kaṇṇāṭipōṟ ṟaṉṉakattē potintuvittu viḷaṅkak kāṭṭum nāṭakam ākiya māntar viḷiyāṭṭut tōṉṟalāyiṟṟeṉṟuṇartal vēṇṭum, nāṭakam eṉṉuñcol viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷi yuṭiyateṉpataṟku māṇikka vācakap perumāṉ aruḷicceyta,
  pukavē takēṉ uṉak kaṉparuḷ yāṉeṉpol lāmaṇiyē!
  takavē eṉiyuṉak kāṭkoṇṭa taṉmiyep puṉmiyari
  mikavē yuyarttiviṇ ṇōrip paṇittiaṇ ṇāvamutē!
  nakavē takumem pirāṉeṉṉi nīceyta nāṭakamē! 
(tiruccatakam, 10)
eṉṉun tiruvācakac ceyyuḷē cāṉṟām. iṉṉum, potu makkaḷ ellārum nāṭakatti āṭṭam eṉṟu vaḻaṅki varutalum uṟṟunōkkaṟpāṟṟu. iṉṉum itu kuti eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṟṟēṉṟiya kūttu eṉṉuñ collāṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, viḷiyāṭṭu, nāṭakam, āṭṭam, kūttu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷellām mutalil orē poruḷi yuṇartuvaṉavāyp, piṉṉar nāṭakamuṟikaḷ palkap palkac, ciṟitu ciṟitu poruḷ vēṟṟumi yuṭiyavāyiṉa veṉka.
iṉi nāṭakam āṭumvaki tuvakkattiliruntē ev vevvāṟu māṟutaleyti varalāyiṟṟeṉpatu ārāyaṟ pāṟṟu. oruvaṉiyum oruttiyiyum vittu maṇam puritalik kāṇuñ ciṟārkaḷ tāmum aṅṅaṉam maṇañ ceyvittu viḷiyāṭat toṭaṅkukiyil, maṇamakaṉukku aṭi yāḷamāka āṇvaṭivāyc camitta oru marappāvi yiṉiyum maṇamakaḷukku aṭiyāḷamākap peṇvaṭivāyc camitta oru marappāviyiṉiyum oruṅkuvittu, avi tamakkut tām aṟintavāṟu āṭiyaṇikalaṉkaḷ alaṅkal cātti oppaṉi ceytu viḷiyāṭal kaṇṭāmaṉṟō? atupōlavē, āṇṭil mutirnta iḷiñarum piṟarun tām mutaṉmutaṟ ṟuvaṅkiya nāṭakattiyum marappāvikaḷ koṇṭē naṭattik kāṭṭiṉar. itu pāvik kūttu eṉṟum pommalāṭṭam eṉṟum iṉṟu kāṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. yām ciṟu piḷḷiyā yirunta kālattil ariccantiranāṭakam muṟṟum aḻakiya pāvik kūttil vittut tiṟampaṭa naṭattappaṭṭa taṉip pārttu makiḻntirukkiṉṟēm. 
iṅṅaṉam marappāviyāṟ camitta pāvikaḷik koṇṭu nāṭakam naṭattappaṭutal pōlavē, tōlāṟ camitta pāvikaḷik koṇṭum nāṭakam naṭattappaṭutal paṇṭik kālantoṭṭē yuḷateṉpatu cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟakkātikku aṭiyārkku nallār uritta vuriyāl naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. āṉālum, iv viruvakik kūttil marappāvik kūttē ēṉit tōṟpāvik kūttukkum muṟpaṭṭa teṉpatu, tōṟpāvik kūttun tolli marappāvi yiyakkamum eṉṉuñ civañāṉa cittic ceyyuḷiṟ paḻamipporuṭarun tolli eṉṉuñ col marappāvik kūttukku aṭimoḻiyāy niṟṟalāl teḷiyappaṭum. eṉavē, marappāvi tōṟpāvikaḷālēyē mutaṉmutal nāṭakam naṭattik kāṭṭappaṭṭateṉpatu uṇarntukoḷka. vaṭamoḻip paḻanūlākiya māpāratattiṉ kaṇṇum  (3,30,23,5,39,1) pāvikkūttu moḻiyap paṭṭiruttalāṉum, āciriyar kuṇāṭṭiyarālē iyaṟṟappaṭṭa pirukatkatiyil acurarkkut taccaṉāṉa mayaṉ eṉpavaṉiṉ putalvi taṉ ṟōḻikkup pāvikkūttuk kāṭṭi avaḷi makiḻvippavaḷ eṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭiruttalāṉum itu vaṭanāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum paṇṭināḷiṟ paraviyiruntami naṉkuṇarappaṭu meṉka.
iṉi, uyiraṟṟa pāvikkūttil vittu māntariṉ ulakiyaloḻukkatti naṭattik kāṭṭuvatu pēruḻip piṉiyum peruvaruttattiṉiyun taruvatallāmalum, uyiruḷḷa makkaḷiṉ kuṇaṅkuṟikaḷi uṇmiyil uḷḷapaṭiyē pulappaṭuttutaṟkum ākāmiyiṉ avviṭarppāṭu kaṇṭu, piṉṉar iḷiñarkaḷiyē nāṭakamāṭap paḻakki avarkaḷ palavakik kōlampūṇṭu oru katiyiṉi naṭattik kāṭṭumāṟu aṟiñar vakuttaṉar. ivvāṟu makkaḷē nāṭakam āṭak kaṟṟukkoṇṭapiṉ, itu mikuntuvarap pāvikkūttu nāḷaṭivil aruki varalāyiṟṟu, eṉṟālum, pāvikkūttu muṟṟumē maṟintu pōkāmal, iṭiyiṭiyē iṉṟukāṟum naṭipeṟṟu varutali orōviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. 
aṟṟēl aḵtāka kūttiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum vēṟupāṭu eṉṉiyeṉiṟ kūtteṉpatu oru katiyiṉ oru pakutiyi oruvarēṉum aṉṟi orucilarēṉum āṭik kāṭṭuvatuṅ, katiyē taḻuvātu oru ceyyuṭ poruḷik kaṇ ki kāl mukam mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷāl āṭik kāṭṭuvatum, viḻākkālaṅkaḷil oruvarēṉum orucilarēṉum pāṭṭuṅ koṭṭuṅkoṇṭu makiḻccimikku āṭuvatum eṉap palatiṟappaṭum. itaṉ virivellāñ cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟu kātikku aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyavuriyiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka. maṟṟu, nāṭakam eṉpatō muḻuvatūuṅ kati taḻuvivarum kūttākum, iḵtu aṭiyārkku nallāruriyāṉum naṉkaṟiyak kiṭakkum. eṉṟālum, nāṭakam eṉṉuñcol orōvoru kāṟ kati taḻuvāta kūttiṉmēṟṟāyum varutal kaḻutoṭu kāṭṭiṭi nāṭakam āṭi eṉṉun tiruvācakattāl (tiruccatakam, 7) viḷaṅkāniṟkiṉṟatu.
2. nāṭaka varalāṟu
iṉi, mutalmutal nāṭakamāṉatu civapirāṉālum umip pirāṭṭiyārālum ākkappaṭṭateṉṟum, civapirāṉāl vakukkappaṭṭatu. tāṇṭavam eṉṉum peyarttāy āṇ makkaḷ mummuramāy āṭuṅ kūttāmeṉṟum, umip pirāṭṭi yārāl vakukkappaṭṭatu. lāyam eṉṉum peyarttāy nilattiṉiṉṟum mēleḻāta aṭikaḷiṉ acivoṭu muṉṉum piṉṉumāka āṭicceṉṟu ki kaṇ puruvam itaḻ mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷāṟ kātal nikaḻcciyiṉip pulappaṭutti iciyuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉitu naṭipeṟuṅ kūttāmeṉṟun tacarūpakaṅ kūṟāniṟkiṉṟatu (1,10) iṅṅaṉamē muttirārāṭcacamum mālatimātavamum nuvalutal kāṇka. kāḷitācarun tāmiyaṟṟiya maṟṟoru nāṭakamākiya māḷavikākki nimittirattiṟ kaṇatācar eṉṉum nāṭaka āciriyar vāyilākac civapirāṉum ammi yumē nāṭakatti mutaṉ mutal ākkiṉōrāva reṉpataṉit teḷivuṟuttal karuttiṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. mēluṅ, ki.mu.700ām āṇṭil aḵtāvatu iṟṟikku 2600 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ iruntavarāka muṭivu ceyyap paṭṭirukkiṉṟa pāṇiṉi muṉivar1 tamatu aṭṭāttiyāyi yiṟ kuṟippiṭṭirukkum naṭa cūttiraṅkaḷ eṉṉum nūl iññāṉṟu nāṭṭiya cātiram eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu, eṉavē, innūl iṟṟikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉamē iyaṟṟappaṭṭa mikap paḻiya nūlātal tāṉē pōtarum. ittuṇip paḻiyatākiya nāṭṭiya cātirattiṉ kaṇ tiripuratakaṉam eṉṉum nāṭakak kāppiyaṅ kuṟippiṭṭiruttalāṟ, paṇṭik kālattirunta muṉṉōrkaḷ mutaṉ mutal āṭakkaṇṭu kaḷittatu civapirāṉ muppuraṅkaḷi eritta kati taḻuviya nāṭakamēyātal teṟṟeṉap pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? akkati taḻuviya nāṭakam attuṇip paḻiyatāy iruttal paṟṟiyaṉṟē piṟkālat tirunta vatcarājaṉ (ki.pi.1163) eṉṉum nāṭakap pulavaṉum, appeyarpūṇṭa tiripuratākam eṉṉum nāṭakak kāppiyat tiṉi iyaṟṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉeṉpatu, eṉṟivvāṟu tamiḻarkaḷ vaṇaṅkiya tamiḻtteyvamākiya civapirāṉum ammiyumē nāṭakatti mutaṉmutal ākkiṉavar kaḷeṉṉum varalāṟu paṇṭutoṭṭu varutalkoṇṭuñ, civapirāṉ muppuram eritta nikaḻcciyē mutaṉmutal nāṭakamākac ceytu naṭittuk kāṭṭap paṭṭateṉṟu vaṭamoḻip paḻanūlākiya nāṭṭiya cāttiraṅ kūṟutal koṇṭum paṇṭikkālattil nāṭakanūl uṇṭāyatu tamiḻmakkaḷuḷḷē tām eṉṟu aṟitalvēṇṭum. tamiḻil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa paḻiya nāṭaka nūlkaḷ iññāṉṟu kāṇakkiṭiyāmi paṟṟiyum, ikkālattu vaḻaṅkum nāṭakak kāppiyaṅka ḷellām āriyam pirākirutam arttamākati marāṭṭi mutalāṉa vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷil eḻutap paṭṭiruttal paṟṟiyum nāṭaka nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻikkē yuriyavi pōlumeṉṟu maliyaṟka. ēṉeṉiṉ, vaṭakkēvantu kuṭiyēṟiya āriyarkaḷ naṭaṉamuṅ kūttum iyaṟṟat terintavarkaḷ allareṉavuṅ, katitaḻīiya nāṭakam paṇṭiyāriyarkkuḷ naṭipeṟṟa teṉpataṟku āriyavēta nūlkaḷuḷ yāṇṭum ētorukuṟippuṅ kāṇappaṭavilli yeṉavum vaṭamoḻiyārāycciyil mikac ciṟantavarākiya kīt (Keith) eṉṉum āciriyar naṉkāyntu muṭivukaṭṭi yiruttalāl2 naṭaṉamum nāṭakamuṅ kūttu mellām mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭaṟintu nūlkaḷ eḻutiṉōr paṇṭit tamiḻāciriyarkaḷēyātal naṉku peṟappaṭāniṟkum. itaṉālaṉṟē toṉṟutoṭṭu iyalum iciyum nāṭakamun tamiḻukkē uriyavātal terittuc cāṉṟōrellān tamiḻi muttamiḻ eṉṟu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟār ivvāṟē āriyattit tritam eṉṉum aṭi koṭuttu vaḻaṅkal vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭā miyiṉ, avvaḻakkut tamiḻukkē ciṟantatātal teḷiyappaṭum eṉpatu.
allatūum, nāṭakatti mutaṟkaṭ ṭōṟṟuvitta iṟivaṉ ataṉip paratamuṉivaṉukkuk koṭuttu, ataṉi avaṉ vāyilāka vaḻaṅka viṭuttaṉaṉ eṉṟu nāṭṭiya cāttiram uri tarutalāl, nāṭakattiṟku mutalāciriyaṉ parataṉē yeṉpatūum naṉku peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. civapirāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vanta muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷēyātalum, avaṉit tīppiḻampoḷiyil vittu vaṇaṅkit tīvaḷartta tamiḻ vakuppiṉar paratarē yātalum ematu māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum nūlil3 virivāka viḷakkik kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ippeṟṟiyarāṉa tamiḻpparata vakuppiṉariṟ ṟōṉṟiya paratamuṉiva ṉoruvaṉukkē civapirāṉāl nāṭakam vaḻaṅkap paṭṭa teṉṉum paṇṭi varalāṟu, paḻiya nāṭakakkali tamiḻmakkaḷuḷḷētāṉ tōṉṟi neṭuka vaḻaṅkivaralāyiṟ ṟeṉṉum uṇmiyiṉi uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉi pōl viḷḷa viḷakkutal kaṇṭu koḷka. allatūum, iññāṉṟu vaṭamoḻikkaṇ uḷḷa nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ īṟṟil nuvalappaṭum vāḻtturiyum paratavākkiyam eṉap peyar kūṟappaṭṭu varutalum itaṟkup piṉṉumoru cāṉṟāy niliyum atuvēyumaṉṟip, pācaṉ eṉṉum nāṭakappulavaṉittavira, avaṉukkuppiṉ vantōrāṉa cūttirakaṉ kāḷitācaṉ, harṣaṉ, pavapūti mutalāṉa vaṭamoḻināṭakap pērāciriya rellārun tām iyaṟṟiya nāṭakakkāppiyaṅkaḷiṉ mutalilum iṭiyilum īṟṟilu mellāñ civapirāṉiyē vaṇaṅkiyum vaḻuttiyuṅ kuṟippiṭṭum iruttalum, ap perumāṉic ciṟanteṭuttu vaḻipaṭun tamiḻp pērāciriyarkaḷē nāṭakam vallārātali naṉkaṟivuṟuttum aṭiyāḷamāmeṉka.
iṅṅaṉañ civapirāṉ muppuraṅkaḷi yeritta kati taḻīip paṇṭutoṭṭu naṭittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭu vanta nāṭakaṅ koṭukoṭṭi eṉṉum peyarttāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum paḻamikkālattē naṭikkappaṭṭu vanta toṉṟām. iṟṟikku 1700 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ aracu vīṟṟirunta ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉuñ cēravēntaṉ, taṉṉip paḻitta vaṭanāṭṭu aracarmēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu, avari yellām pōril veṉṟu, ciṟippaṭuttik kaṇṇakiyiṉ uruvañ camittataṟku imayamalik kaṇ āynteṭutta karuṅkalli avar talimītēṟṟik koṇarvittut, taṉ māpperun tēviyōṭum nāṭaka araṅkēṟi nāḷōlakkam irunta ñāṉṟu paṟiyūrk kūttaccākkiyaṉ eṉṉum nāṭakam vallāṉ, civapirāṉ muppurameritta koṭukoṭṭi, nāṭakattiyē avar muṉṉiliyil naṭittukkāṭṭi avari makiḻvittaṉaṉeṉṟu, appōtu avarōṭu oruṅkiruntu ataṉik kaṇṭa āciriyar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ.
  āṅkavaḷ taṉṉuṭaṉ aṇimaṇi araṅkam
  vīṅkunīr ñālam āḷvōṉ ēṟit
  tirunilic cēvaṭic cilampuvāy pulampavum
  paritaru ceṅkiyiṟ paṭupaṟi ārppavuñ
  ceṅkaṇ āyiran tirukkuṟip paruḷavuñ
  ceñcaṭi ceṉṟu ticimukam alampavum
  pāṭakam patiyātu cūṭakan tuḷaṅkātu
  mēkali oliyātu meṉmuli aciyātu
  vārkuḻi āṭātu maṇikkuḻal aviḻātu
  umiyavaḷ orutiṟa ṉāka ōṅkiya
  imiyavaṉ āṭiya koṭṭic cētam
  pāttaru nālvaki maṟiyōr paṟiyūrk
  kūttac cākkiyaṉ āṭaliṉ makiḻntu.
(cilappatikāram, naṭukaṟkāti, 65-77)
eṉṟu aruḷicceytirukkumāṟṟāṟ, koṭukoṭṭi nāṭakan tamiḻnāṭṭiṉkaṇṇum āṭappaṭṭu vantami naṉkaṟiyappaṭu meṉpatu.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, paṇṭittamiḻ moḻikkaṇ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nāṭakakkāppiyaṅkaḷum, akkāppiya ilak kaṇakkūṟum nāṭakat tamiḻnūlkaḷuñ cilappatikāra uri yāciriyarāṉa aṭiyārkku nallār kālattilēyē aiyakō! iṟantu pōyiṉa! avi aṅṅaṉam iṟantupaṭṭatu etaṉāleṉiṟ kūṟutum; ici nāṭakaṅkaḷi aruvaruppārāṉa putta camaṇcamayattavarkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟum perun tiraḷāyp pōntu ittamiḻ nāṭeṅkaṇuṅ kuṭi pukuntu vikiya ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal, avar taṅ kōṭpāṭukaḷiṟ cikkuṇṭa tamiḻāciriyarkaḷum avarpōl avaṟṟi aruvaruttu viṭavē annūlkaḷellām aṅṅaṉam aḻintupaṭalāyiṉa! avarkkuppiṉ civacamayatti vaḷarttu vanta āciriyarkaḷum, īraneñcam aṟṟa putta camaṇ tuṟavoḻukkamuṟikaḷit taḻīit tamakkup paṇṭēyuriya viḻumiya aṉpiṉintiṇi oḻukkaviyalkaḷi orīit tāmum  avarpōl ici nāṭakat tamiḻik kinnekiḻa viṭṭaṉar! iṅṅaṉam tam nāṭṭuṭpukum ayalavar oḻukalāṟukaḷ ettiṟattavāy iruppiṉum avi  tammi ārāyātu kippaṟṟit, tamakkuriya cīrtta paṇṭiyurimikaḷi yellām eḷitil iḻantu viṭutaṟkaṇ muṉ niṟpā rāṉa nan tamiḻmakkaḷiṭiyē icināṭakat tamiḻ iṟantupaṭṭatu iyalpēyaṉṟō? ici nāṭakam maṭṭumō! iyaṟṟamiḻ naṅkiyuṅkūṭat taṉ taṉicciṟappi varavara iḻantu, ayalmoḻic coṟporuḷ nañcu taṉ kaṇ aḷaviṉṟi viravappeṟṟuk kuṟiyuyiruṭiyaḷāyt, taṉṉi muṉpōl arukāp pēriḷamiyiṟ peruka vikkum ōr āṇmip perumakaṉ utaviyi vēṇṭiniṟṟal aṟiyātār yār? atuniṟka.
iṉit, teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭavar nili ivvāṟāyiṉum, vaṭanāṭṭil āriyamoḻiyit taḻīi niṉṟa nam muṉṉōr nili attuṇi iraṅkat takkatāyilli. vaṭanāṭṭilirunta aṟiñar āriyamoḻiyi vaḻaṅkap pukuntiṭiṉun, tamakkuriya ici nāṭakaṅkaḷiyum orē teyvak kōṭpāṭṭiyuṅ kiviṭārāy, avi tammi mutalvittē iyal ici nāṭaka nūlkaḷuñ camaya ārāycci nūlkaḷum piṟavum vaṭa moḻikkaṭ ṭoṭarpāka iyaṟṟi varalāṉār. eṉṟivvāṟu vaṭamoḻik kaṇ nāṭakanūl vanta varalāṟṟiṉ uṇmi aṟintukoḷka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	immuṭivi ematu māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum eṉṉum nūliṉ 729ām pakkam mutaṟ kāṇka.
2.	Read Dr. A. B. Keith’s ‘The Sanscrit Drama, pp.25-27.
3.	māṇikkavācakar kālam pakkam, 16 164 - 67.	
3. nāṭaka amippu
iṉi, nampōṉṟa makkaḷuṭ cilar tamatiyaṟkikkun tamatu collukkun tamatu oḻukalāṟṟiṟkun tamatu iṉpa nukarccikkum ottu niṟpārōṭu uṟavukoṇṭum, avaṟṟukku māṟupaṭṭu niṟpāroṭu pakikoṇṭum oḻukāniṟpuḻi, avartam vāḻnāḷ nikaḻccikaḷ oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu piṇintu cikkuṇṭu, īṟṟil ac cikku viṭupaṭṭu naṉṟāy muṭitaluñ, cikku viṭupaṭātu tītāy muṭitaluṅ kāṇṭu maṉṟē. iṅṅaṉam iruvēṟu vakippaṭṭu muṭiyum makkaḷiyaṟki nikaḻccikaḷiṉ toṭarpē katiyeṉṉum peyarttāy nāṭaka araṅkiṉkaṇ vittu āṭuvārāl naṭattik kāṭṭappaṭuvatākum. eṉavē, nāṭakattiṟku uyirāvatu oru ciṟanta katiyēyā meṉpatūum, akkatiyiṉ kaṭ ṭoṭarpupaṭṭu niṟkum makkaḷiyum makkaḷ nikaḻccikaḷiyum nāṭaka araṅkiṉkaṭpōntu āṭikkāṭṭu vār aṅṅaṉam āṭikkāṭṭutaṟku icinta muṟiyāl ellām vittu oru nallicip pulavaṉāl toṭukkap paṭuvatē nāṭakakkāppiyamā meṉpatūum iṉitu peṟappaṭum.
aṟṟēl, katinūlukkum nāṭaka nūlukkum vēṟupāṭu eṉṉiyeṉiṟ, katinūl eḻutum āciriyaṉ taṉ uriyākak kūṟavēṇṭuvaṉa vellāṅ kūṟutaṟku uriyaṉ, maṟṟu, nāṭaka nūlāciriyaṉō, nāṭakakkatiyuḷ varuvār peyarum, avar varutalpōtalum, avarvarum iṭamuṅkālamuṅ kuṟippataṉṟi, aṅṅaṉam varuvār iyaṟki ceyaṟkikaḷum avar varutalpōtal vakikaḷum avarvarum iṭaṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟki kālattiṉ nilikaḷun tāṉē nērniṉṟu virittuk kūṟutaṟku uriyāṉ allaṉ aṉṟi avikaḷik kūṟa viḻikuvāṉēl, tāṉ iyaṟṟum nāṭakakkatiyil varuvār vāyilniṉṟē avi tammic curukkamāka varuvippāṉallatu, tāṉē avi tammik kūṟappeṟāṉ, katinūlāciriyaṉō tāṉ variyuṅ katiyuḷ varuvāriṉ vēṟākat taṉṉit taṉiniṟuttit, taṉakkumuṉ celvārāka avarit taṉṉetir toṭarpupaṭa niṟutti avartam iyaṟki ceyaṟkikaḷiyum avartam varutal pōtal  vakikaḷiyum avar iyaṅkum iṭattiṉiyaṟki kālattiṉ nilikaḷiyum tāṉ vēṇṭumā ṟellām virittēyāyiṉuñ curukkiyē yāyiṉum variyappeṟuvaṉ. mēlum, oru nāṭakakkati nikaḻcciyāṉatu ōr iravil oṉpatu maṇikkut tuvaṅki iraṇṭallatu mūṉṟumaṇikkuḷ naṭattik kāṭṭutaṟku icintatāka irukkavēṇṭumātalāl, atu virintu cellutaṟku iṭampeṟātu; aṉṟiyum. mika virinta pāratak kati irāmāyaṇak kati kantapurāṇak kati mutaliyavaṟṟi nāṭakamāka naṭattik kāṭṭappukuvārum, avi tammip pala pakutikaḷākap pirittu, av vovvoru pakutiyiyum ovvōriravil muṭikkattakka paṟpala nāṭakaṅkaḷāka iyaṟṟi naṭattik kāṭṭak kāṇṭumākaliṉ, nāṭakameṉa vakukkappaṭuva vellām ōriravil naṭattik kāṭṭutaṟku ēṟṟa curuṅkiya aḷavuṭiyatākavē iruttal vēṇṭumeṉ ṟuṇarntu koḷka. ivvaḷavu kaṭantu iyaṟṟappaṭuvaṉa nāṭakamuṟiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭālum, avi ōriravil āṭik kāṭṭutaṟku iciyāmiyiṉ nāṭakamātal cellāteṉka. maṟṟuk, katinūṟkatikaḷō aṅṅaṉam āṭik kāṭṭappaṭuvaṉa allavāy, ettaṉi nāṭkaḷēṉun toṭarntu payilutaṟkēṟṟa amippuṭiya vākaliṉ, avi āciriyaṉ vēṇṭu māṟellām virintu cellutaṟ kēṟṟa peṟṟiyavā meṉṟu kaṭippiṭikka.
iṉṉum, nāṭakam eṉpatu parantupaṭṭa in nilavulakattiṉ oru pakutiyilō aṉṟic cila pakutiyilō naṭipeṟum makkaḷ cilar palariṉ iyakkat toṭarpi vari yaṟippaṭṭa oru ciṟiya iṭamākiya araṅkiṉ kaṇṇē aḻaku mikuttu āṭikkāṭṭun tiṟaṉuṭiyatu; attiṟattiṉi niṉintu pārkkuṅkāl, oru pulnuṉimēl niṉṟa oru ciṟupaṉit tivaliyāṉatu taṉṉic cūḻavuḷḷa periya iyaṟkipporuṭ ṭōṟṟaṅkaḷit taṉṉakat taṭakkik kāṭṭun tiṟattiṉiyē ataṟku oppākac collalām. maṟṟu, virinta oru katinūlō aṅṅaṉamē virinta makkaḷiyal nikaḻccikaḷit taṉkaṇ nīḷakkāṭṭun taṉmiyuṭiyatu ataṟku mika akaṉṟatōr aḻakiya ērinīr, taṉakkumēl muḻunilā vaṭṭamiṭṭu vayaṅka ataṉic cūḻa vāṉmīṉ ṟokutikaḷ poṉṟukaḷeṉa miḷirat tōṉṟum oru peru nīla vāṉparappit taṉkaṇ viḷaṅkak kāṭṭutaliyē uvamiyākac collalām. ivvāṟu aviyiraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumi yuṇarntu koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
iṉip, paṇṭināḷil nāṭakaṅkaḷ ceyyuḷum uriyum viraviya naṭiyil ākkappaṭṭaṉa. maṟṟuk, katinūlkaḷō ceyyuḷnaṭi oṉṟilēyē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭakaṅkaḷ ēṉ aṅṅaṉañ ceyyuḷum uriyuṅ kalanta naṭiyil ākkap paṭṭaṉa veṉiṉ avi, ulakattil nikaḻum makkaḷ oḻukalāṟu kaḷip perumpālum aṅku nikaḻumāṟē yeṭuttu āṭik kāṭṭa vallārāl ōr araṅkiṉkaṇ vittu naṭattik kāṭṭappaṭun taṉmiyavātalāl, ulakattiṟ kalviyaṟivilum āṟṟalilum mikka mēṉmakkaḷiṉ uri yāṭṭukaḷic ceyyuḷ naṭiyilum, avarallāta ēṉi makkaḷiṉ uriyāṭṭukaḷi avaravar uyarvu tāḻvukkēṟṟa urinaṭiyilumāka iyaṟkikku māṟākāmal naṭattik kāṭṭiṉāl maṭṭumē avaṟṟi nēriruntu kāṇpārkku iṉpam uṇṭām; ākavē, nāṭakaṅkaḷellām iyaṟkineṟi tiṟampāmal aṅṅaṉam iruvaki naṭiyum oruṅkuvirāy amikkappaṭalāyiṉa. maṟṟuk, katikaḷi nuvaluṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷō, araṅkiṉkaṇ vittu avi tammi naṭattik kāṭṭuvaṉa allavāy, iyaṟki ceyaṟkikaḷi yeṭuttu virikkun tam āciriyaṉṟaṉ aḵkiyakaṉṟa aṟiviṉṟiṟaṅkaḷiyē perumpāṉmiyum pulappaṭuttuvaṉavāyt, tammi ūṉṟip payilvārkkuc coṟcuvi poruṭcuvikaḷip perukuvikkum peṟṟiya vākaliṉ, avitam iyalukku ēṟpac ceyyuḷ naṭivaḷam oṉṟē poruntalāyiṉa veṉka. eṉavē, kāḷitācarum avarkku muṉ, piṉ ṉirunta nāṭaka āciriyaru mellān tām toṭutta nāṭakakkatiyuḷ varuvār uyarvu tāḻvukkēṟṟa ceyyuḷnaṭi urinaṭikaḷ viraviya muṟiyiṟ ṟammuṭiya nāṭakanūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiṉārāka, viyācarum vāṉmīkiyum avarpōṉṟa ēṉik kāppiya nūlāciriyaru mellān tām nuvaluṅ katikaḷit tam aṟiviṉ ṟiṟaṉāl ettuṇic cuvi peruka nuvalalāmō attuṇikkēṟpa muḻutuñ ceyyuḷ naṭiyilēyē yākkalāyiṉā reṉṟuṇarntukoḷka kāḷitācarum nāṭakamuṟi viṭuttuk, kāppiyamuṟi taḻīik kati nūl yākkap pukkuḻic, ceyyuḷnaṭi yoṉṟaṉiyē mēṟkoṇṭār eṉpataṟku, avariyaṟṟiya irakuvamcam, kumāracampavam mutaliya poruṭṭoṭarnilic ceyyuṭkaḷē cāṉṟā meṉpatu. 
4. nāṭakak katiyamippu
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam amikkappaṭum nāṭakam eṉṉum ōr aḻakiya uṭampukku uyirpōlvatu, ataṉūṭu toṭarntu nikaḻuṅ katiyē yākum. valla ōr uyir illāta veṟṟuṭampu ētoru payaṉum uṭiyatākāmi pōlak, katiccuvi nirampāta nāṭakamum payaṉuṭiya tākātu ēṉeṉṟāṟ, katiccuvi vāyāta nāṭakattik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟuvār ulakil evarumē ilarākaliṉeṉpatu.
aḵtokkum; katiccuvi yeṉpatu iṉṉateṉ ṟaṟintilamākaliṉ, ataṉi oruciṟitu viḷakkik kāṭṭuka veṉiṟ, kāṭṭutum. ōr araciḷiñaṉ, taṉ nakarattiṟkup puṟampēyuḷḷa kāṉakattil ulavum puli karaṭi muḷḷampaṉṟi yāṉi mutalāṉa maṟavilaṅkukaḷ taṉkīḻ vāḻuṅ kuṭimakkaṭkut tīṅku iḻiyāvāṟu, tiṅkaḷukku orukāl taṉ ēvalāḷar cilaruṭaṉ paṭikkalan tāṅkik kutiri yūrntuceṉṟu, ataṉkaṇ vēṭṭamāṭit tirumpuvāṉ eṉṟa aḷavumaṭṭum oru ciṟukati oruvar colvarāyiṉ ataṉik kēṭpār ciṟitum iṉpuṟār. maṟṟu, ataṉi avvāṟu kūṟātu, aṅṅaṉam vēṭṭam āṭit tirumpum vaḻakkam uṭiya avviḷiñaṉ orukāl ak kāṉakattiṉūṭu koṭiyatoru muḷḷam paṉṟiyip piṉṟoṭarntu tāṉ ivarnta kutiriyi virintu celuttic celvuḻit, taṉṉuṭaṉ pōnta ēvalāḷarip pirintu, ataṉpiṉṉē akkāṭṭiṉūṭu neṭuntolivu pōyviṭṭāṉ. avaṉmuṟ ceṉṟa paṉṟiyō avaṉ kaṇkaḷukkum eṭṭāmal aṭarnta putarkaḷiṉūṭu maṟintōṭiviṭṭatu. annēram pakalavaṉ vāṉ ucciyil eṟikkum vēṉil naṇpakal, tāṉun taṉkutiriyum neṭuvaḻi vantamiyāl mey viyirttu nāvaṟaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟu, mēṟcella māṭṭāmal ōriṭattil ayarntu niṟkavēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatāyiṟṟu. uṭaṉē, avaṉ taṉ kutiriyiviṭṭuk kīḻiṟaṅki, ataṉi aruku ōṭiya mali nīraruviyil nīr parukkik kariyiṟ pulmēyaviṭṭut, tāṉun taṉatu viṭāy taṇittu oru maranīḻalil amarntāṉ. amarntāṉukku ayarviṟi kaṇṇuṟakkam vara aṅkuk koḻumiyāy vaḷarntirunta pulliṉmēṟ paṭuttāṉ; paṭuttuc ciṟitu nērattilellān taṉṉi maṟantu tuyilvāṉāyiṉaṉ ivaṉ ivvāṟirukka, ak kāṉakattil ulavuṅ koṭiya vēṅkippuli yoṉṟu iritēṭi ap pakkamāy vantatu. aṅkē pulmēyuṅ koḻuviya ak kutiriyik kaṇṭu, ataṉik koṉṟu tiṉṟaṟku vāyit tiṟantavāṟāy ataṉmēṟ pāya muyalkiyil, ak kutiri verukkoṇṭu ōṭit, tuyiṉṟu koṇṭirunta taṉ araciḷiñaṉ arukē cārntu kaṉikka, avaṉuṅ katumeṉa viḻitteḻuntu niṉṟu cūḻa nōkkiṉāṉ. itaṟkuḷ appuli taṉ muyaṟcikku iṭiyūṟu viḷippavaṉāy eḻuntuniṉṟa av viḷiñaṉik kāṇṭalun, tāṉ ak kutirimēṟ pāyvatu viṭuttu, av viḷiñaṉmēṟ pāyntatu. aḵtaṅṅaṉam pāytaliyun, tuyiṉṟeḻunta taṉ talivaṉ taṉatu vēṟpaṭi nilattiṟ kiṭappat tāṉ veṟuṅ kiyaṉāy niṟṟaliyuṅ kaṇṭa ak kutiri ōr imip poḻutil taṉ ṟalivaṟkum avaṉ mēṟ pāyum appulikkum iṭiyē pāyntatu. itu kaṇṭa iḷiñaṉ oru kutiyiṟ kīḻkkiṭanta taṉatu vēṟpaṭiyik kuṉinteṭuttu, ataṉi mikavun tiṟamiyākac cuḻaṟṟi ap puliyiṉ mukattiṉmēl ōcci, ataṉik koṉṟu vīḻttiṉāṉ. innērattil avaṉ ṟaṉ ēvalāḷarum avaṉpāl vantu cērntu akkutiriyiṉ naṉṟi mikka āṇmiyiṉiyun, tam araciḷiñaṉ ṟaṉ pērāṇmi yiṉiyum viyantu, vīḻttappaṭṭa ap peruvēṅkip puliyiṉiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu, ellārumāyt tam nakarukkut tirumpiṉar eṉṟu av varaciḷiñaṟku orunāḷ nikaḻnta viyattaku nikaḻcciyiṉi yeṭuttuk kūṟiṉāl, ataṉik kēṭpārkku, naṉṟiyun tikippum irakkamum accamum aiyamum muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkiyūm viyappum makiḻcciyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākat tōṉṟi avarip peritum iṉpuṟuttumaṉṟō, yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ, kuṭi makkaḷip pātukākkum oru nalla aracaṟkup putalvaṉāy vaḷaviya celvavāḻkkiyil uṟiyum ōr araciḷiñaṉ taṉ nalattik karutātu taṉ kuṭimakkaḷ nalattiyē karuti maṟavilaṅkuḷi vēṭṭamāṭac ceṉṟā ṉeṉpatu kēṭṭu naṉṟiyuṇarvum, aṅṅaṉañ ceṉṟavaṉ accantarattakka akkāṉakattiṉūṭu taṉ ēvalāḷariyum pirintu taṉiyē cellumāṟu nērntatu eṉpatu kēṭṭut tikip puṇarvum, orunāḷum nīrviṭāyum pacittuṉpamuṅ kaḷippum aṟiyā tāṉ kaṭuvēṉil naṇpakalil ak kāṭṭiṟ ṟaṉicceṉṟu viṭāyttu varuntiṉāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭu irakkavuṇarvum, ayarntuṟaṅkum av viḷiñaṟku aṇittāka maṟavēṅki oṉṟu vantateṉpatu kēṭṭu accavuṇarvum, avvēṅki kutirimēṟ pāytaloḻintu av viḷiñaṉmēṟ pāyap pukka teṉpatu kēṭṭu iḵtu yātāy muṭiyumō eṉṉum aiyavuṇarvum, iḵteṅṅaṉam muṭiva tāyiṟṟeṉa aṟiyaviḻiyum vēṭkiyuṇarvum, av vēṅki taṉ talivaṉ mēṟ pāyap pukuvatu kaṇṭa ak kutiri taṉakku varum ūṟupāṭṭiyum oru poruṭpaṭuttātu avaṟkum ataṟkum iṭiyē pāyntami kēṭṭum annoṭiyil avviḷiñaṉ taṉ vēṟpaṭiyi eṭuttōcci ap puliyik koṉṟu vīḻttami kēṭṭum viyappoṭu kalanta makiḻcci yuṇarvum, av viḷiñaṉip pirinta ēvalar avaṉpāl vantu cērntu ellārumāyt tamatu nakarkku iṉitupōyc cērntami kēṭṭu āṟutal kalanta makiḻcciyuṇarvum oṉṟaṉ piṉṉoṉṟāyt tōṉṟi ikkati kēṭpārip peritum iṉpuṟuttukiṉṟamiyi ṉeṉpatu. ivvāṟu oru kati nikaḻcci ciṟitāyiruppiṉum peritā yiruppiṉun taṉṉik kēṭpārkku mēṟkāṭṭiya palvaki yuṇarvukaḷi eḻuppun takittāy amikkappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ tām aruḷicceyta tirucciṟṟampalakkōvi yāril mēl uritta ciṟukatiyit,
   tīvāy uḻuvi kiḻittatantō ciṟitē piḻippit
  tāvāmaṇivēl paṇikoṇṭavāṟiṉṟor āṇṭakiyē
eṉṟu aḵkiya aṟivāl iraṇṭaṭiyiṟ ṟiṟampaṭak kuṟittu aṟivuṟuttaruḷiṉami kaṇṭu iṟumpūteytuka. eṉavē, oruvaṉ allatu oruttiyatu oḻukalāṟu ētōr iṭi yūṟṟāṉun taṭippaṭātu ceṉṟu muṭiyumāyiṉ ataṉi   eṭutturippatum ataṉik kēṭpatuṅ kūṟuvārkkuṅ kēṭpārkkum viyappiṉāl nikaḻum iṉpavuṇarviṉip payavāmiyiṉ aḵtu oru katiyākat toṭukkaṟpāla taṉṟeṉpatūum, avvāṟaṉṟi iṭiyūṟukaḷāl tākkuṇṭu naṉṟākavō tītākavō muṭiyum oruvaratu oḻukalāṟē irutiṟattārkkum viyappiṉiyum iṉpavuṇarviṉiyun tantu katiyākat toṭukkappaṭutaṟkurittāmeṉpatūum iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkumeṉka.
aṟṟēl, aṅṅaṉam iṭiyūṟukaḷāl tākkuṇṇum oruvaratu oḻukalāṟu maṭṭumē viyappiṉiyum iṉpat tiṉiyun tarutal eṉṉiyeṉiṉ ōr yāṟṟu veḷḷattiṉ virivum viriviṉmiyum valivum valiviṉmiyum ataṉik kuṟukkiṭṭu niṟkum aṇiyiṉ uraṅkoṇṭē aṟiyap paṭutalpōlavum, oru neruppiṉ vaṉmiyum meṉmiyum ataṉāl erikkappaṭum oru kāṉakattiṉ aḷavupaṟṟiyē uṇarappaṭutalpōlavum, oru cūṟikkāṟṟiṉ kaṭumiyun taṇivum ataṉāl, alittu muṟikkappaṭum maraṅkaḷiṉ nilipaṟṟiyē aḷantaṟiyappaṭutal pōlavum oruvaruṭiya aṟivāṟṟal uṭalāṟṟalum maṉanalamum aviyiṉmiyu mellām avaratu oḻukalāṟṟi vantu iṭimaṟikkum iṭi yūṟukaḷiṉ vaṉmi meṉmi vāyilākavē naṉkaḷantaṟiyap paṭumeṉka. aṅṅaṉam vantu maṟikkum iṭiyūṟukaḷukkum añcātu avaṟṟi mēṟkaṭantu celvāṉṟaṉ āṇmi yiṉik kaṇṭu viyantu makiḻtalum, maṟṟu avviṭi yūṟukaḷi etirntum avaṟṟi mēṟkoḷa māṭṭātu ayarntu māḻkuvāṉik kaṇṭu iraṅki varuntutalum, iṉi avi tammik kaṇṭu añciyōṭu vāṉik kaṇṭu iḻittu ikaḻtalum makkaḷ ellār māṭṭum iyaṟkiyāy nikaḻak kāṇṭumaṉṟē. ākavē, at taṉmiyavām iṭiyūṟukaḷāl tākkappaṭātavaṉṟaṉ uramum uramiṉyum aṟitaṟku vēṟu viḻumiya vāyil oṉṟuṅ kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ, ap peṟṟiyāṉṟaṉ oḻukalāṟu makkaḷevarkkum viḻumitām iṉpam payavāmiyum iyaṟkiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟṟāṟ, kati kaḷeṉṟaṟkuc ciṟantaṉa iṭiyūṟṟiṉ vāyilāka oruvaṉṟaṉ allatu oruttitaṉ uḷḷa niliyiṉip pulappaṭutti, avvāṟṟāl ataṉip payilvārkkuṅ kāṇpārkkuṅ kēṭpārkkum iṉpa vuṇarviṉi eḻuppum nīravā meṉṟu teḷintu koḷka.
iṉi, iccākuntala nākaṭattiṉkaṇ nuvalappaṭuṅ kati nikaḻcciyum iṭiyiṭiyē vantu kuṟukkiṭun taṭikaḷāl annikaḻccikkuriyāriṉ iyaṟki nilikaḷip pulappaṭutti avaṟṟi yuṇarvārkku accamum viyappum muṭivaṟi vēṭkiyum makiḻcciyum payantu avari iṉpuṟuttumāṟu oruciṟitu kāṭṭuvām. tuṣiyantavēntaṉ tērilamarntu villuṅkaṇiyum ēnti oru māṉip piṉṟoṭarnta vaṇṇamāy oru kāṉakattiṉūṭu virintu celkiṉ ṟuḻit tuṟavōr cilar iṭiyē pukuntu ammāṉiṉik kolla lākāteṉṟu maṟikka, aracaṉum avaratu kaṭṭaḷikkuk kīḻppaṭintu ataṉik kollātu viṭṭu viṭukiṉṟāṉ. itaṉāl, aracaṉ ōruyirik kolla muṉintu niṉṟavaḻiyum, aṟavōr collukku aṭaṅki ataṉi avvāṟu ceyyātu viṭṭa maṉanalaṉ uṭiya ṉātal pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? iṉṉum, naṭuvē pukunta tuṟavōr, mēl naṭipeṟavēṇṭuṅ kati nikaḻccikkum vaḻi ceykunarāy niṟṟalum aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu; yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, avarkaḷ avvaracaṉik kaṇṇuvar āciramattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkē cakuntaliyāl viruntēṟkap paṭumāṟu ēvukiṉṟamiyiṉeṉpatu.
iṉi, avvāciramattiṟkuḷ nuḻinta avvaracaṉ taṉakkuc ciṟitu toliviṟ cakuntaliyum avaṭaṉ tōḻi māruṅ kuṭaṅkaḷil mukanta nīri iḷañceṭikaḷukku viṭṭuk koṇṭu uriyāṭi varutaliyum, avarkaḷ aḻakil mikkavarāy iruttaliyuṅ kaṇṭu, avarkaḷpāl uṭaṉē cellutaṟku māṭṭāṉāy, maracceṟivukaḷiṉ maṟivil niṉṟapaṭiyāy avarkaḷi nōkki avaruriyāṭṭukaḷiyuṅ kēṭkiṉṟāṉ. innērattil oru vaṇṭu cakuntaliyiṉ mukattaṇṭi paṟantu vantu avaḷit tuṉpuṟuttā niṟka, attuṉpattiṉiṉṟu taṉṉi viṭuvikkumāṟu avaḷ taṉ tōḻimārkaḷi vēṇṭiyaḻikkiṉṟāḷ. tōḻimārō, tamatu nāṭṭukku aracaṉāṉa tuṣiyantaṉ aṇṭiyil iruttali aṟiyārā yiruntum, pakaṭikkāka nammiyellāṅ kākkum aracaṉ tuṣiyantaṉik kūppiṭu! eṉkiṉṟaṉar; eṉṟatum, maṟivi lirunta aracaṉ taṉṉit terivittuk koḷḷutaṟku atutāṉ takkanērameṉak karuti avvaṇṭi ōccuvāṉpōl ataṉi ataṭṭiya vaṇṇamāy avarmaruṅku aṇikiṉṟāṉ. pārmiṉkaḷ aracaṉatu iyakkattiṟkut taṭiyāyp pukunta tuṟavōr avaṟkuc cakuntaliyik kāṭṭiṉar cakuntaliyiṉ iyakkattiṟkut taṭiyāyp pukunta oru vaṇṭu avaṭku aracaṉik kāṭṭiṟṟu. iṉṉum, piṉ nikaḻnta taṭiyāṉatu cakuntali ciṟituvarun tuṉpattiṟkum añci veruḷum meṉṟaṉmi yuṭiya ḷeṉpataṉiyum, ittuṇi melliyal puṭiyāḷ iṉit taṉ kaṇavaṉāl taṉakku viḷiyun tuṉpatti eṅṅaṉan tāṅkuvāḷeṉpa taṉiyum aṟivittal kāṇka. ivviruvakit taṭikaḷāl iṭi maṟikkappaṭṭuc cellum ikkati nikaḻcci, taṉṉip payilvārkku viyappu, muṭivaṟiyum vēṭki, irakkam mutaliya vuṇarvukaḷik kiḷaṟi makiḻcci ceytal uṇarntukoḷka.
iṉṉum, aracaṉ mēṟ colliyavāṟu cakuntaliyiṉ tōḻimārōṭu uriyāṭikkoṇṭu tāṉ cakuntali mēṟkoṇṭa kātalip pulappaṭuttiyuñ, cakuntali taṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kātali uyttaṟintun tāṉum avarum iṉpuṟṟu niṟkiyil, akkāṉakattiṉūṭu oru kāṭṭiyāṉi ataṉi yuḻakkikkoṇṭu varutali yuṇarntu aṅku iyaṅkuvār iṭum peruntikilōci avaratu nēya nikaḻccikku iṭiyūṟāyp pukuntu? ammakaḷiriyum avvaracaṉiyum vēṟupirittu, itaṉip payilvārkku accattiṉiyum, piṉ ikkatinikaḻcci yātāy muṭiyumō eṉa muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkiyiṉiyum viḷivittu niṟṟa laṟintu koḷka.
mēlum, aṅṅaṉañ cakuntaliyic caṭutiyiṟ pirintu taṉ kūṭārattuṭ ceṉṟuviki avaḷvayappaṭṭa niṉiviṉaṉāy, maṟittum avaḷpāṟ cellutaṟku vāyileṉṉi yeṉṟu ārāyntu varuntikkoṇṭirukkum aracaṉpāl, mīṇṭum avvāciramattiluḷḷa tuṟavikaḷ cilarpōntu, kaṇṇuva muṉivar varumvariyil tāmvēṭkum vēḷvikaṭku arakkarāl iṭukkaṇ uṇṭākātavāṟu avaṟṟip pātukākkaveṉṟu vēṇṭa, aracaṉum ataṟkuṭaṉē iyintu, mīḷat tāṉ cakuntalipāṟ cellutaṟku ataṉiyē ēṟṟatoru vāyilākap paṟṟutal kāṇka. mīṇṭum nērnta ittuṟavōr vēṇṭukōḷ, iṭiyaṟunta maṇikkōvip poṟ caraṭṭiṉi iṇikkum poṉ ācupōl, iṭi viṭṭa ikkati nikaḻcciyiṉit toṭarpu paṭuttutaliṉ, iḵtu itaṉ muṭivaṟiyum vēṭki kiḷarntārkkup perumakiḻcciyiṉip payattal tērntukoḷka.
iṉṉun, tuṟavōriṉ vēṇṭukōḷukku iṇaṅki aracaṉ mīṇṭuṅ kaṇṇuvaratu pāḻikku (āciramattiṟku)c cellat tuvaṅkukiyil, aracaṉṟaṉ aṉṉi taṉatu nōṉpu muṭiviṉkaṇ taṉṉōṭu uṭaṉirukkumāṟu aracaṉik kālan tāḻātu nakarukku mīḷka eṉak kaṭṭaḷi viṭuttami, piṉṉum aracaṟkōr iṭiyūṟāyp pukutal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam maṟittum pukunta iṭiyūṟṟi aracaṉ taṉ naṇpaṉākiya vitūṣakaṉi nakarkkup pōkku māṟṟal nīkkiviṭutaliṉ, ituvuṅ kati nikaḻcciyiṉi mīṇṭun toṭarpupaṭuttuṅ karuviyāyp payilvāri iṉpuṟuttutal aṟintukoḷka.
atumēyumaṉṟi, iraṇṭāmvakuppiṉ īṟṟil, in nāṭaka  āciriyaṉ mēl varuṅ kati nikaḻccikku oruperuṅ kāraṇamākat tantirukkuṅ kuṟippum peritum viyakkattakkatā yirukkiṉṟatu. mēlē, cakuntaliyi muṟṟum maṟantuviṭṭa aracaṉpāṟ, kaṇṇuvarāl viṭukkappaṭṭuc ceṉṟa avaḷ, avaṉ taṉṉik kāṉakattil maṇanta varalāṟukaḷi yellām ettuṇiyō eṭutturittum, avaṉ avikaḷi niṉivu kūrarkkillāṉāy avaḷi veṟuttu otukkiya piṟ cilanāḷiṟ kiṭitta taṉatu kaṇiyāḻiyik kaṇṭu, avaḷit  tāṉ maṇanta varalāṟukaḷiyellām niṉivukūrntu taṉ nēyaṉākiya vitūṣakaṉ avvaralāṟukaḷit taṉakku niṉivūṭṭāmi eṉṉi yeṉṟu avaṉi varuntik kēṭṭalum, avvitūṣakaṉ uṇmiyil eṉakku attuṟavi makaḷiṭattil evvakiyāṉa viruppamum illi. *** nāṉ pakaṭiyāyc coṉṉa coṟkaḷi uṇmiyāka niṉiyātē eṉṟu aracaṉ iraṇṭām vakuppiṉ īṟṟilē kāṉakattiṟ ṟaṉakkuc colliya coṟkaḷi eṭuttu moḻintami kāṇka. ākavē, mēlnikaḻuṅ katinikaḻcci yiṉi muṉṉuṇarntu, ataṟkutaviyāka aracaṉ vāymoḻiyil vittuk kāḷitācar iraṇṭām vakuppiṉ īṟṟiṟ kūṟiya coṟkaḷiṉ payaṉ peritum niṉivukūrntu viyakkaṟpālatā meṉka. 
iṉṉum, aracaṉ cakuntaliyi maṇantu cilanāḷ vikip, piṉṉart taṉ nakarkku ēki, avaḷit taṉpāl aḻittukkoḷḷa viḻintavaṉ, atu ceyyātu avaḷi muṟṟumē maṟantupōy viṭṭāṉ. ivvaḷavil innāṭakam niṉṟu viṭumāyiṉ, aracaṉ avaḷi aṅṅaṉam aṟavē maṟantupōyatu eṉṉi yeṉṟum, avaṉāl aṅṅaṉaṅ kiviṭappaṭṭa cakuntaliyiṉ vāḻkki piṉṉar evvāṟu āyiṟ ṟeṉṟum aṟiya māṭṭāmiyiṟ payilvōr uḷḷam amitipeṟātu ataṉāl innāṭakañ cuvikuṉṟik kuṟipāṭu uṭiyatākum. ākavē, āciriyar kāḷitācar, cakuntali māṭṭup peruṅkātal koṇṭavaṉākiya avvaracaṉ avaḷi aṅṅaṉam eḷitilē maṟantuviṭutal iyalāmi yuṇarntu, avaṉ avaḷi muṟṟum maṟantu pōtaṟku oruperuṅkāraṇaṅ kaṟpippārāyt, turuvācar cāpatti innāṭakattiṉ nāṉkām vakuppiṉ tuvakkat tilēyē varuvittal kāṇka. taṉ āruyirk kātalaṉiyē niṉintu avaṉvayamākiya uḷḷattiṉaḷāyt, taṉṉiyun taṉṉiccūḻa  nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷiyum oruṅkēmaṟantu, ayarntu taṉkuṭil vāyilil amarntirunta cakuntali, caṭutiyil viruntiṉarāyp pōnta turuvācariṉ varukiyiyum uṇarātiruppavē, avar avaḷmēṟ cīṟṟaṅkoṇṭu niṉmaṉam evaṉvayappaṭṭu evaṉiyē niṉintu koṇṭirukkiṉṟatō, avaṉ *** niṉivūṭṭap paṭṭālum niṉṉi niṉiyā toḻika eṉṟu vaci (cāpam) kūṟic ceṉṟa oru nikaḻcciyiṉi āciriyar īṇṭup paṭittu moḻintamiyum, ivvaḷavilum ak kati nikaḻcciyiṉi aṟuttu niṟuttāmal, niṉivu kūrtaṟku aṭiyāḷamāṉa ōr aṇikalattik kāṇṭalum avvacavu nīṅkum eṉṟu atu tīrtaṟkum vaḻi ceytu, cakuntali mīṇṭun taṉ kātalaṉik kūṭuvatākiya nikaḻcci yiṉit toṭarpupaṭuttu vittamiyumākiya aruntiṟaṉ peritum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatoṉṟāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
atuvēyumaṉṟi, mēṟ coṉṉavāṟu nērnta turuvācar vaciyiṉic cakuntali aṟivaḷāyiṉ, avaḷ taṉ kiyil aracaṉ aṇinta kaṇiyāḻiyiṉpāṟ karuttu mika vuṭiyaḷāy atu taṉ kiyiviṭṭu naḻuvāmaṟ pātukāttu oḻuku vaḷākaliṉ, aracaṉ avaḷi maṟattalum nērātu. atu nērātākavē, mēṟ kati nikaḻcciyuñ, cakuntaliyiṉ maṉat tiṭpattiyuṅ kaṟpiṉ kaṭappāṭṭiyum ūṭuruvi oḷira māṭṭātāyc cuvikuṉṟi yoḻiyum. ākavē, āciriyar kāḷitācar attuṇik kuṟipāṭukaḷum itaṉkaṇ ēṟāmi vilakkutaṟ poruṭṭu īṇṭucceyta ceykittiṟam nuṭpam niṉiyun tōṟum pēruvakiyiṉi ūṭṭāniṟkiṉṟatu. turuvācar varukiyiṉiyum, avar kūṟiya vacimoḻi yiṉiyum aṟinta tōḻimār, avitammi aṟiyātirunta cakuntalikku avaṟṟi aṟiviyāmalē viṭumāṟu ceyta āciriyaratu nuṭpa viṉittiṟaṉ, katiyiṉ ṟoṭarpi uḷ nuḻintu kaṇṭu iṇikkamāṭṭārkku viḷaṅkātāyiṉum, ataṉi avvāṟu kaṇṭu toṭukka vallārkkup periyatōr iṟumpūtiṉip payakkumeṉka.
mēlum, ēḻuvakuppukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭa innāṭakak katic cuviyāṉatu, tuvakkattiliruntu paṭippaṭiyē uyarntu naṭuniṟkum nāṉkām vakuppiṉkaṇ iṉi yuyartaṟku elli illiyāmaḷavil vantu niṟṟalum, ataṉ kaṭ collappaṭṭa turuvācar vacimoḻiyāṉatu oru maṇik kōviyiṉi iṇikkuṅ kokkuviṟpōla muṉpiṉ kati nikaḻcciyiṉit toṭarpupaṭuttuṅ karuviyāy uṟutalum niṉiviṟ patikkaṟ pālaṉavākum. in nāṭakattiṉip payilvār itaṉ naṭuniṟkum nāṉkām vakuppiṉi nōkkic celluṅkāṟ cakuntali taṉ kaṇavaṉāl maṟantuviṭappaṭṭut taṉiyāḷāyt tuyarkūrntu niṟkum niliyiṉi niṉintu kavaliyum, uyarntōr niliyiṟṟappār eṉpataṉāl āṟutalum, iṉṉāmi vantakkāl iṟivaṉiyē tuṇiyākap paṟṟutalun, turuvācar vacimoḻiyiṉi yuṇarntu accamum, atu tīrtaṟkām maruntiṉi aṟiyappeṟutalāl maṉattērcciyuṅ, karuk koṇṭu kaṇavaṉāl maṟakkappaṭṭa cakuntaliyiṉpāl irakkamum, avaḷ tantiyār avaḷiyum avaḷkūṭiya yāḻōr maṇattiyum makiḻntēṟṟami terintu uvakiyuñ, cakuntaliyik kaṇavaṉ illattiṟku ēkuvittal vēṇṭi av vāciramattiluḷḷa makaḷir avaṭku maṅkaḷa vāḻttuc ceyyum muṟikaṇṭu oruvaki makiḻcciyun tōḻimārum avaḷ tantiyum avaḷiviṭṭup piriyuṅkāṟ paṭum āṟṟāmiyāl maṉa urukkamum, avaḷ tantiyār avaṭkuk kūṟum aṟivuriyiṉ vāymiyāl ilalṟavuṇarcciyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyt tōṉṟiniṟṟaluṇarntu iṉpuṟumāṟu āciriyar itaṉi amitta tiṟam peritu peritu
itaṉpiṉ, taṉpāṟpōnta cakuntali taṉ kātaṉ maṉiviyē eṉpataṉi niṉivukūrātu avaḷi aracaṉ vilakkiyavuṭaṉ avaḷ ōr arampi mātiṉāl eṭukkappaṭṭu vāṉvaḻiyē maṟintu pōyiṉāḷ eṉa moḻiyum avvaḷavil in nāṭakak kati muṭikkappaṭumāyiṟ, piṉṉum atu payilvārkku muṭivaṟiyum vēṭkiyiṉit taṇiyāmi yiṟ kuṟipāṭu uṭittām. akkuṟipāṭu nīkkutaṟku āṟām vakuppiliruntu āciriyar ikkatiyiṉi naṭāttuntiṟaṉ ciṟitu kāṭṭutum. cempaṭavaṉ oruvaṉ piṭitta mīṉ oṉṟaṉ akaṭṭiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa kaṇiyāḻi oṉṟu tuṣiyanta maṉṉaṉ peyar cetukkappaṭṭatā yiruttal kaṇṭu kāvalāḷar ataṉi avvaracaṉpāṟ koṇarntu koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. aracaṉ taṉ peyar poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ataṉik kāṇṭalun, turuvācar iṭṭa vacimoḻiyiṉ tītu nīṅkap peṟṟāṉāyt, taṉṉāl vilakkappaṭṭa cakuntaliyit tāṉ kātaṉmaṇam parintukoṇṭañāṉṟu tāṉ avaḷatu kiviralil aṇinta kaṇiyāḻiyē ata veṉṟum, avaḷ taṉpāl varukiṉṟuḻi vaḻiyilirunta oru vāvi nīril avaḷtāṉ aṟiyātapaṭiyē aḵtu avaḷ kiyi viṭṭu naḻuvi viḻuntatākalvēṇṭameṉa avaḷuṭaṉ pōnta kutami ammiyār kūṟiyatu vāymiyē yākal vēṇṭumeṉṟun, tāṉ avaḷi maṇanta varalāṟukaḷi avaḷ taṉmuṉṉē eṭutturikka avaṟṟit tāṉ poyyeṉak karutiyatellām niṉiviḻappē yeṉṟum naṉku niṉivukūrntu aracaṉ āṟṟoṇāt tuyarameytip pulampum vakikaḷiyollām āciriyar itaṉ āṟām vakuppiṉkaṇ melleṉa nuvaṉṟu mellac cellutal kāṇka.
iṉṉum, innāṭakak kati nikaḻcciyiṉip paṭippaṭiyē uyarttuk koṇṭuceṉṟu, nāṉkām vakuppiṉ iṟutiyilē ōr āṟṟāmikku iṭaṉāy ataṉi ucci yilēṟṟiṉa āciriyar, piṉṉar aintām vakuppilē mikkatoru tuyarattil atu kīḻ iṟaṅkumāṟu ceytu, ataṉ īṟṟil ataṉik karikāṇāta tuyarkkaṭalil amiḻttuvārāyk, katit taliviyākiya cakuntaliyiyē kaṭpulaṉukkut teṉpaṭā vāṟu vāṉūṭu maṟiyac ceyta tiṟaṉ ōrntukoḷka.
aṅṅaṉaṅ katittaliviyiyun talivaṉiyun tuyark kaṭaluḷ amiḻtti, attuṇiyil ataṉiviṭṭup pōtal nallicippulami yākāmiyiṉ, attuyark kaṭaluḷ amiḻntār aṉivariyum ataṉiliruntum mēleṭuttu viṭutaṟku āṟām vakuppil āciriyaṉ ceyta cūḻccit tiṟaṉi iṉṉuñ ciṟitu kāṭṭutum, talimakaḷ talimakaṉ avark kuriyāri yellām peruntuyarkkaṭaluṭ paṭuppittaṟku ētuvāy niṉṟatu, aracaṉ cakuntali kiyiliṭṭa kaṇiyāḻi uṟṟanērattil avaḷatu kiviraliṉiṉṟuṅ kaḻaṉṟu oru peruntaṭattiṉuḷ amiḻntip pōṉa nikaḻcciyēyaṉṟō? aṅṅaṉam āḻntāri yellām ataṉilirunteṭuttu iṉpakkari cērkkaluṟuvār, muṉṉē tāṉum āḻntu piṉṉē ellāriyum iṭark kaṭaluḷ āḻttiya ak kaṇiyāḻiyiṉi mutaṟkaṇ mēleṭuttalē ceyaṟpālatām. tāṉ āḻttiya poruḷi mēleṭutta viṭavē, ataṉāl āḻntā rellārum mēluyarntu karicērntiṉpuṟṟiruttal tāṉākavē nikaḻum. ākavē, āciriyar kāḷitācar āṟām vakuppiṉ tuvakkattilēyē, akkaṇiyāḻiyiṉi mēṟkoṇaralāyiṉāreṉka.
aṅṅaṉam ataṉi mēṟkoṇarumiṭattum, aintām vakuppiṉ iṟutiyil tuṉpattāl ūṭuruvappeṟṟu makiḻcciyi iḻantārkku, ataṉi mīṇṭum varuvittu avaruḷḷatti āṟṟutalaṉṟō ceyaṟpālatām īṇṭu āciriyaṉum aṅṅaṉañ ceytatuṇṭōveṉiṉ uṇṭu. tāṉ piṭitta mīṉiṉ vayiṟṟik kīṇṭakkāl ataṉilirunteṭutta aracaṉatu kaṇiyāḻiyi vili ceyyak koṇarnta cempaṭavaṉi ūrkāvaṟkārarum avarkkut talivaṉāṉa kottavālum piṭittuk koḷḷa, avaṉum avarum uriyāṭum pakutikaḷ nakiccuvi payattaluṭaṉ, avaṉik koṭumiyāka naṭattāmaṟ kottavāl avaṉ vāymoḻi yiṉ uṇmi kaṇṭu avaṉukku aracaṉiṭamiruntu paricu vāṅkik koṭuttu, avaṉuṭaṉ kēṇmi pārāṭṭic cellum pakutiyum mutaṟkaṭṭōṉṟiya nakiccuviyiṉic citiyātāyp payilvāruṭaṉ ellāriyum makiḻavittal kāṇka. nāṉkām vakuppiliruntu uḷḷaṅkuḻiyun tuṉpattiṉ pakkamāyt tirumpi, aintām vakuppiṉ īṟṟiṟ peruki nirampiya tuyarkkaṭaluṭ pōntu viḻunta innāṭakakkati nikaḻcciyi āṟāmvakuppiṉ tuvakkattilēyē ataṉkaṇṇiruntum eṭuttu nakiyum makiḻvum mēṉmēṉmikumāṟu ataṟku mīṇṭum uyirppēṟṟik koṇṭuceṉṟu, ēḻām vakuppiṉkaṇ āciriyaṉ ataṉi iṉitu muṭikkum ariya periya pulamittiṟaṅ kaṇṭu iṉpuṟuka.
iṉi, innāṭakak katinikaḻccikku ōr accāṇi pōl iṭippukuntu niṉṟa turuvācar vacimoḻikku orumīṉi aṭiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyuñ, cakuntali tuṣiyantaṉi iṭi niṉṟu piṇikkuṅ kātal aṉpiṟku oru kaṇiyāḻiyi aṭiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyuṅ, kātalarāvār tammi oruṅku piṇippitta iṟivaṉ tiruvaruṭṭuṇiyiṉi nāṭātu tām oruvariviṭṭu oruvar pirinta nilimiyiṉiyē niṉintu tuyarkūrntu niṉṟāl avaratu uḷḷantiṭpam iṉṟi nīrpōl nekiḻnta patattatāyiruttaliṉ attakiya vuḷḷattiyē piṟariṭṭa vacimoḻi paṟṟiyirukkum eṉpataṟku ammīṉ irukkum oru taṭākanīri aṭiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyun, taṉatu tuṇiyi etirpārāta kātalarkkum avaratu aṉpiṉ mikutiyip pārāṭṭi iṟivaṉ ṟiruvaruḷē orukāṟ ṟuṇiyāyp pōntuniṉṟu avaratuḷḷattip paṟṟiya vacimoḻiyiṉip pōḻntu ataṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa avaratu kātalaṉpiṉi mīḷvittu miḷirac ceyyum eṉpataṟkut taṟceyalāyt tāṉ vīciya valiyiṟ cikkuṇṭu pōnta oru mīṉiṉ vayiṟṟip pōḻntu ataṉkaṇṇiruntum eṭutta aracaṉatu kaṇiyāḻiyi veḷikkoṇarnta oru cempaṭavaṉatu ceyali aṭiyāḷamāka niṟuttiyum āciriyaṉ innāṭakatti ākkiyirukkum nuṇmāṇ nuḻipula viyalpu uyttuṇarntu kaḷikkaṟ pālatā meṉka.
iṉik kaṇiyāḻiyi kāṇāmuṉ aracaṉāl vilakkuṇṭu vāṉūṭu maṟintu ēkiya cakuntali evvāṟu āyiṉāḷ eṉavuṅ, kaṇiyāḻiyik kaṇṭapiṟ cakuntaliyit tāṉ maṇanta varalāṟukaḷellām oru ciṟitum viṭāmal niṉivu kūrntu āṟṟoṇāt tuyar koṇṭa aracaṉ evvā ṟāyiṉāṉ eṉavum avar tam muṭivu aṟiyum vēṭki aṭaṅkappeṟātāy itaṉip payilvōruḷḷan tuyaruṟumākaliṉ, ataṉiyum viṭuvittaṟku āciriyaṉ itaṉ āṟām vakuppiṉ īṟṟilum, ēḻām vakuppiluñ ceyyuñ ceykit tiṟaṉiyum īṇṭuk kāṭṭutum.
aracaṉ taṉatu kaṇiyāḻiyik kaṇṭatumutaṟ cakun taliyiyaṉṟip piṟitu etaṉiyum niṉiyamāṭṭāṉāyk kaḻiperuntuyar kūrntu, avaḷatu vaṭivaḻaki ōr ōviyamāka varintavāṟē taṉatu iḷamarakkāvil iruppuḻip, piḷḷip pēṟiṉṟi iṟantupōṉa peruñ celvarāṉa oru vaṇikariṉ ceyti kēṭṭut, taṉakkum putalvaṟpēṟiṉmiyāl taṉatu nilimiyum aṅṅaṉantāṉ ākap pōkiṉṟateṉṉum eṇṇan tōṉṟa āṟṟakillā iṭumpiyuṟṟu ayarntu meymmaṟantu kiṭakkiṉṟāṉ. annērattil, ō pārp pāṉik kāppāṟṟu pārppāṉik kāppāṟṟu eṉṉum ōlak kuraloli caṭutiyil eḻavē, aracaṉ uṭaṉē uṇarvukūṭi yeḻuntu, taṉ naṇpaṉum vitūṣakaṉumāṉa mātaviyaṉ eṉpāṉ kaṭ pulaṉākāta oru koṭumpēyāl mēleṭukkap paṭṭu araṇmaṉiyiṉ uccittaḷattiṉkaṇ vittuk kaḻuttu muṟikkappaṭukiṉṟāṉ eṉpa taṟintu, annoṭiyē villuṅ kaṇiyum ēnti appēyit toṭarntu araṇmaṉimēṟ cella, avaṉetirē intiraṉ tērppākaṉāṉa mātali varukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉik kāṇṭalum aracaṉ ampi villiṉiṉṟum eṭuttuviṭṭu, avaṉ vantaceyti kēṭṭaṟintu, intiraṉukkup pakivarāṉa ōr arakkar kūṭṭattit tolikkumporuṭṭu avaṉatu vēṇṭukōṭpaṭiyē avaṉ koṇarnta vāṉavūrtiyil ēṟi intiraṉatu vāṉnakarkkuc celkiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum ittuṇiyil ikkati nikaḻcciyiṉi āciriyar āṟām vakuppiṉ kaṭṭoṭarpupaṭut tiruttaliṉ, itaṉip payilvāratu uḷḷam aracaṉṟaṉ āṟṟā nilimiyiṉi yaṟintu, aḵtevvāṟu āyiṟṟeṉa mēlum aṟitaṟku avāviya vēṭkiyiṉi iḵtu oru ciṟitu taṇivu ceytal kāṇka.
aṟṟāyiṉum, āṟām vakuppiṉkaṇ oru ciṟitu taṇivu ceyyappaṭṭa muṭivaṟiyum vēṭki, vāṉulakiṟ ceṉṟa vēntaṉ evvāṟāyiṉāṉ eṉa aṟitaṟku mēluṅ kiḷarntu niṟkumākaliṉ, ataṉi muṟṟun taṇivuceytal yāṇṭeṉiṉ ataṟ kaṉṟē ēḻām vakuppu eḻunta teṉpatu ēḻām vakuppiṉ tuvakkattilēyē, tuṣiyanta maṉṉaṉ arakkarkaḷit tolittu avvāṟṟāl tēvēntiraṟkup pērutavipurintu, avaṉ taṉakkuc ceyta peruñciṟappukkaḷi ēṟṟu, avaṉatu vāṉavūrtiyil ivarntu maṇṇulakukkut tirumpukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu nuvalappaṭṭamiyāl, aracaṉatu vāṉ celavi aṟivāṉ vēṇṭi yeḻunta vēṭkiyiṉi āciriyaṉ curuṅkiya kūṟṟilēyē taṇittāṉāyiṟṟu.
iṉic, cakuntaliyiṉ piṟkāla niliyiṉi aṟitaṟku muṉintu niṉṟa vēṭkiyiṉit taṇivuceytu, avvāṟṟāl innāṭakak kati nikaḻcciyiṉi iṉitu muṭikkum āciriyaṉṟaṉ aṟivāṟṟal uṇaraṟpāṟṟu. vāṉvaḻiyē maṇṇulaku nōkkik kīḻ iḻintuvarun tuṣiyantaṉ avvaḻiyiṟ ṟaṉakketirē poṉṉoḷi tulaṅkat tōṉṟiya ēma kūṭam eṉṉum mali kāciyapa muṉivar tam maṉiviyoṭu tavam puriyum perumivāynta teṉpatu mātali collak kēṭṭu, am muṉivari vaṇaṅkutaṟku viḻintu ammalik kaṇ iḻintu aṅkuḷḷa tavappaḷḷikkuc celkiyil iṭiyē viḷiyāṭik koṇṭirunta carvatamaṉaṉ eṉṉuñ ciṟuvaṉ cakuntalikkun taṉakkum piṟanta makaṉē yeṉpa tuṇarntu, piṉṉar aṅkuḷḷa muṉivar makaḷirāl taṉkaṇavaṉ vantaceytiyaṟintu taṉakketirē vanta cakuntaliyik kaṇṭu āṟṟāmi mikku varunta, aṅṅaṉamē cakuntaliyum neñcam nekkurukik kaṇṇīr cintap, piṉṉart tāṉum avaḷum putalvaṉum mātali aḻippac ceṉṟu kāciyapariyum avartam maṉiviyāriyum vaṇaṅki avar tam aruḷpeṟṟu, vāṉavūrtiyil mīṇṭum ēṟit, taṉatu nakarkkup pōntu iṉitu vāḻntaṉaṉ eṉa āciriyar innāṭakak katiyiṉi muṟṟun toṭarpupaṭutti muṭittup, payilvāratu uḷḷattiṉ vēṭkiyit taṇivuceytu, avari mika makiḻac ceyta tiṟaṉ kaṇṭu viyantiṭuka.
ivvēḻām vakuppiṉkaṇ āciriyar viyappum makiḻcciyum āṟṟāmiyum amitiyum aṭuttaṭuttut tōṉṟumāṟu kati nikaḻcciyiṉit tiruppit tiruppic celuttuñ ceykit tiṟaṉi eṉṉeṉpēm! kāciyaparatu tavappaḷḷiyiṉ arukē aracaṉ ciṟitu amarntirukkiyiṟ, caṭutiyil oruciṟāṉ oru ciṅkak kuṭṭiyi iḻutta vaṇṇamāy muṉivar makaḷir iruvaruṭaṉ varutalum, aracaṉ avaṉik kāṇṭalum avaṉmītu viḻivu mikku muḷi yilēyē avaṉiṭattuk kāṇappaṭum āṇmiyiṉi viyantu avaṉ piṟappiṉi ārāytalum, aṅṅaṉam ārāykiyil avaṉ tuṣiyantaṉukkuñ cakuntalikkum piṟantamakaṉ eṉpati ammuṉivar makaḷir avaṉōṭu uriyāṭuñ coṟkaḷi liruntum, aracaṉ acciṟuvaṉatu kiyiṉiṉṟuṅ kaḻaṉṟu kīḻviḻunta kāṇṭakattit tāṉ taṉ kiyileṭuttum ataṉāl avaṉ ūṟupaṭātiruttal koṇṭu, ammakaḷir iṟumpūtuṟṟu uritta urikaḷiliruntum acciṟāṉ taṉ makaṉē eṉṟu tuṇitalum, innikaḻcciyellām am makaḷirāl aṟintu pōnta cakuntaliyum aracaṉum oruvari yoruvar nōkki āṟṟāmiyuṟutalum piṟavum ettuṇi nuṭpamāka ettuṇi aḻakāka ettuṇi iṉimiyāka āciriyaṉāl iṭiyē toṭuttamikkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa! iṅṅaṉam nāṭakak kāppiyam amittaliṟ kāḷitācaraṉṟip piṟar evar vallār! eṉka. 
5. nāṭakamāntar iyaṟki
iṉi, oru nāṭakak kati nikaḻcciyil talivaṉ taliviyarāyum, avarkku naṇpar pakivar notumalarāyun toṭarpupaṭṭu niṟpār ilvaḻi, annāṭakamun toṭarnta kati nikaḻcci illatāy muṭiyum aḵtilliyākavē aḵtoru nāṭakamātalum illiyām. ākavē, oru nāṭakattiṟkut talivaṉ taliviyarum avaroṭu toṭar puṭiyārum iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuva reṉpatu.
